> DEVELOPER

Implement the following plan:

# Experience → VAE Training Pipeline

## Context

Cohezion's FLUME VAE trains on **synthetic gaussian noise** (0.5 ± 0.15) because no real data pipeline exists. Meanwhile, the compound executor captures rich execution experiences (12D trajectories, coherence, anomaly scores, alignment metrics) and persists them to Parquet, SurrealDB, and vault JSON — but none of this feeds back into the VAE.

This pipeline closes the loop: collect real agentic experiences, encode them as 256D vectors, and train the VAE on actual behavior distributions. The trained VAE can then generate novel plausible experiences for universe simulation.

## 256D Encoding Scheme

```
Dims [0:12]   — 12D axiomatic trajectory (from JourneyTracker)
                0:novelty 1:logic 2:field 3:spatial 4:temporal
                5:precipitation 6:coherence 7:efficiency 8:convergence
                9:smoothness 10:resonance 11:harmony

Dims [12:24]  — 12 scalar execution metrics (from CompoundExecutor)
                12:phi_score 13:anomaly_score 14:misalignment_score
                15:intent_confidence 16:duration_norm(log) 17:tokens_norm(log)
                18:cache_hit_rate 19:success(0|1) 20:token_efficiency_norm
                21:quality_score 22:convergence_trend 23:cost_norm

Dims [24:29]  — 5 operation type one-hot
                24:generate 25:analyze 26:search 27:transform 28:persist

Dims [29:256] — 227 semantic fingerprint (deterministic SHA-256 hash expansion)
```

## Files to Create

### 1. `src/cohezion/flume/experience_encoder.py`
- `ExperienceEncoder` class with `encode(experience: dict) -> np.ndarray` (returns 256D float32)
- Deterministic semantic fingerprint via SHA-256 (same approach as JourneyTracker._text_to_latent)
- Log-scale normalization for duration and tokens
- Operation types match JourneyTracker: generate, analyze, search, transform, persist

### 2. `src/cohezion/flume/experience_collector.py`
- `ExperienceCollector` class with `collect_all() -> list[dict]`
- Tier 1: Read `data/journeys/shard_*.parquet` — extract `state_trajectory` (list of 12D vectors) + metadata
- Tier 2: Query SurrealDB `mission_journey` table (non-blocking, skip if unavailable)
- Tier 3: Walk `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/experiments/**/*.json` vault files
- Each tier normalizes records into a common schema dict with required keys

### 3. `src/cohezion/flume/experience_dataset.py`
- `ExperienceDataset(Dataset)` — torch Dataset returning `torch.Tensor` shape `(256,)`
- Constructor: `__init__(experiences: list[dict], seed: int = 42, augment: bool = False)`
- Pre-encodes all experiences at init (fast __getitem__)
- Optional gaussian noise augmentation for regularization
- Compatible with existing `FlumeVAETrainer.train(dataset=...)` hook

### 4. `src/cohezion/flume/experience_pipeline.py`
- `ExperienceTrainingPipeline` class orchestrating: collect → encode → train → checkpoint
- `async run(min_real: int, max_samples: int, epochs: int, ...) -> Path`
- Graceful fallback: if real data < min_real and synthetic_fallback=True, pad with SyntheticFlumeDataset
- Uses existing `TrainConfig(z_dim=256)` and `FlumeVAETrainer(config).train(dataset=...)`

### 5. `scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py`
- CLI entry point with argparse
- Args: --min-real, --max-samples, --epochs, --batch-size, --lr, --session, --seed, --no-synthetic
- `asyncio.run(pipeline.run(...))`

### 6. `src/cohezion/skills/EXPERIENCE_VAE_TRAINING_PRIME.md`
- PRIME skill definition codifying the full pipeline pattern

### 7. `tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py`
5 focused tests:
1. `test_encoder_output_shape_and_determinism` — encode same experience twice → identical 256D
2. `test_encoder_operation_type_onehot` — each op type activates correct dim
3. `test_dataset_returns_correct_tensor` — dataset[0] is torch.Tensor shape (256,)
4. `test_collector_handles_missing_dirs` — empty parquet/vault dirs → empty list, no crash
5. `test_pipeline_trains_with_synthetic_fallback` — full pipeline with no real data → checkpoint saved

## Files to Modify

### `src/cohezion/flume/__init__.py`
- Add exports: `ExperienceEncoder`, `ExperienceDataset`, `ExperienceTrainingPipeline`

## Key Design Decisions

1. **256D not 12D VAE**: Encode full experience context (trajectory + metrics + semantics), not just spatial position. Preserves compatibility with existing 256D VAE architecture.

2. **Deterministic fingerprints**: Use SHA-256 hash expansion (like JourneyTracker does), not Python `hash()` which varies across sessions.

3. **Actual FlumeVAETrainer API**: Use `TrainConfig(z_dim=256, ...)` + `FlumeVAETrainer(config).train(dataset=custom_ds)` — the existing hook.

4. **Parquet schema**: Handle actual schema where trajectories are in `state_trajectory` field as nested float lists.

5. **Non-blocking SurrealDB**: try/except around all SurrealDB calls; skip tier if DB unavailable.

## Verification

```bash
# Run tests
uv run pytest tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py -v

# Run full pipeline (will use synthetic fallback since little real data exists)
uv run python scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py --epochs 5 --min-real 1

# Verify checkpoint was created
ls data/flume/checkpoints/flume_vae_ep5.pt
```

     1→"""Training pipeline for the FLUME autoencoder (VAE).
     2→
     3→Trains the FlumeEncoder to compress and reconstruct latent vectors
     4→with a combined loss: MSE reconstruction + KL divergence + coherence
     5→regularization toward HIHO 0.5 target.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import logging
    11→import time
    12→from dataclasses import dataclass
    13→from pathlib import Path
    14→
    15→import numpy as np
    16→import torch
    17→import torch.nn as nn
    18→import torch.optim as optim
    19→from torch.utils.data import DataLoader
    20→
    21→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass
    25→class TrainConfig:
    26→    """FLUME VAE training configuration."""
    27→
    28→    z_dim: int = 256
    29→    batch_size: int = 64
    30→    epochs: int = 50
    31→    lr: float = 1e-3
    32→    kl_weight: float = 0.1
    33→    coherence_weight: float = 0.05
    34→    grad_clip: float = 1.0
    35→    lr_schedule: str = "cosine"  # "cosine" or "step"
    36→    checkpoint_dir: str = "data/flume/checkpoints"
    37→    log_interval: int = 10
    38→    data_dir: str = "data/mass_sim/artifacts"
    39→    max_samples: int = 100_000
    40→
    41→
    42→class FlumeVAETrainer:
    43→    """Train a simple VAE on FLUME latent vectors.
    44→
    45→    Since the full FlumeEncoder requires a tokenizer, this trainer
    46→    operates on a simpler encoder/decoder that works directly on
    47→    latent vectors (bypassing text tokenization).
    48→
    49→    Architecture:
    50→        Encoder: z_dim -> hidden -> mu, log_var (z_dim each)
    51→        Decoder: z_dim -> hidden -> z_dim (reconstruction)
    52→    """
    53→
    54→    def __init__(self, config: TrainConfig | None = None) -> None:
    55→        self.config = config or TrainConfig()
    56→        self.device = torch.device("cpu")  # No CUDA on Strix Halo
    57→
    58→        z = self.config.z_dim
    59→        hidden = z * 2
    60→
    61→        # Encoder: outputs mu and log_var for VAE
    62→        self.encoder = nn.Sequential(
    63→            nn.Linear(z, hidden),
    64→            nn.ReLU(),
    65→            nn.Linear(hidden, hidden),
    66→            nn.ReLU(),
    67→        ).to(self.device)
    68→        self.mu_head = nn.Linear(hidden, z).to(self.device)
    69→        self.logvar_head = nn.Linear(hidden, z).to(self.device)
    70→
    71→        # Decoder
    72→        self.decoder = nn.Sequential(
    73→            nn.Linear(z, hidden),
    74→            nn.ReLU(),
    75→            nn.Linear(hidden, hidden),
    76→            nn.ReLU(),
    77→            nn.Linear(hidden, z),
    78→        ).to(self.device)
    79→
    80→        self._all_params = (
    81→            list(self.encoder.parameters())
    82→            + list(self.mu_head.parameters())
    83→            + list(self.logvar_head.parameters())
    84→            + list(self.decoder.parameters())
    85→        )
    86→
    87→    def _reparameterize(self, mu: torch.Tensor, log_var: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    88→        """VAE reparameterization trick: z = mu + std * eps."""
    89→        std = torch.exp(0.5 * log_var)
    90→        eps = torch.randn_like(std)
    91→        return mu + std * eps
    92→
    93→    def _forward(
    94→        self, x: torch.Tensor
    95→    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
    96→        """Full VAE forward pass. Returns (reconstruction, mu, log_var)."""
    97→        h = self.encoder(x)
    98→        mu = self.mu_head(h)
    99→        log_var = self.logvar_head(h)
   100→        z = self._reparameterize(mu, log_var)
   101→        recon = self.decoder(z)
   102→        return recon, mu, log_var
   103→
   104→    def _loss(
   105→        self,
   106→        x: torch.Tensor,
   107→        recon: torch.Tensor,
   108→        mu: torch.Tensor,
   109→        log_var: torch.Tensor,
   110→    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, float]]:
   111→        """Compute VAE loss: MSE + KL + coherence regularization."""
   112→        # Reconstruction loss
   113→        mse = nn.functional.mse_loss(recon, x)
   114→
   115→        # KL divergence
   116→        kl = -0.5 * torch.mean(1 + log_var - mu.pow(2) - log_var.exp())
   117→
   118→        # Coherence regularization: penalize mu mean deviating from 0.5
   119→        mu_mean = mu.mean(dim=-1)
   120→        coherence_loss = torch.mean((mu_mean - 0.5) ** 2)
   121→
   122→        total = (
   123→            mse
   124→            + self.config.kl_weight * kl
   125→            + self.config.coherence_weight * coherence_loss
   126→        )
   127→
   128→        metrics = {
   129→            "mse": mse.item(),
   130→            "kl": kl.item(),
   131→            "coherence_loss": coherence_loss.item(),
   132→            "total": total.item(),
   133→        }
   134→        return total, metrics
   135→
   136→    def train(self, dataset: torch.utils.data.Dataset | None = None) -> list[dict]:
   137→        """Run training loop.
   138→
   139→        Parameters
   140→        ----------
   141→        dataset : Dataset, optional
   142→            Training dataset. If None, loads from config.data_dir.
   143→
   144→        Returns
   145→        -------
   146→        list[dict]
   147→            Per-epoch training metrics.
   148→        """
   149→        if dataset is None:
   150→            from cohezion.flume.dataset import FlumeTrajectoryDataset
   151→
   152→            dataset = FlumeTrajectoryDataset(
   153→                data_dir=self.config.data_dir,
   154→                max_samples=self.config.max_samples,
   155→                z_dim=self.config.z_dim,
   156→            )
   157→
   158→        loader = DataLoader(
   159→            dataset,
   160→            batch_size=self.config.batch_size,
   161→            shuffle=True,
   162→            drop_last=True,
   163→        )
   164→
   165→        optimizer = optim.Adam(self._all_params, lr=self.config.lr)
   166→
   167→        if self.config.lr_schedule == "cosine":
   168→            scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.CosineAnnealingLR(
   169→                optimizer, T_max=self.config.epochs
   170→            )
   171→        else:
   172→            scheduler = optim.lr_scheduler.StepLR(optimizer, step_size=20, gamma=0.5)
   173→
   174→        checkpoint_dir = Path(self.config.checkpoint_dir)
   175→        checkpoint_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   176→
   177→        epoch_metrics: list[dict] = []
   178→        logger.info(
   179→            f"Starting FLUME VAE training: {self.config.epochs} epochs, "
   180→            f"{len(dataset)} samples, batch_size={self.config.batch_size}"
   181→        )
   182→
   183→        for epoch in range(1, self.config.epochs + 1):
   184→            epoch_start = time.perf_counter()
   185→            batch_metrics: list[dict] = []
   186→
   187→            for batch in loader:
   188→                x = batch.to(self.device)
   189→                recon, mu, log_var = self._forward(x)
   190→                loss, metrics = self._loss(x, recon, mu, log_var)
   191→
   192→                optimizer.zero_grad()
   193→                loss.backward()
   194→                torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self._all_params, self.config.grad_clip)
   195→                optimizer.step()
   196→
   197→                batch_metrics.append(metrics)
   198→
   199→            scheduler.step()
   200→
   201→            # Aggregate epoch metrics
   202→            epoch_avg = {
   203→                k: np.mean([m[k] for m in batch_metrics]) for k in batch_metrics[0]
   204→            }
   205→            epoch_avg["epoch"] = epoch
   206→            epoch_avg["lr"] = scheduler.get_last_lr()[0]
   207→            epoch_avg["elapsed_s"] = time.perf_counter() - epoch_start
   208→            epoch_metrics.append(epoch_avg)
   209→
   210→            if epoch % self.config.log_interval == 0 or epoch == 1:
   211→                logger.info(
   212→                    f"Epoch {epoch}/{self.config.epochs} | "
   213→                    f"MSE: {epoch_avg['mse']:.4f} | "
   214→                    f"KL: {epoch_avg['kl']:.4f} | "
   215→                    f"Coh: {epoch_avg['coherence_loss']:.4f} | "
   216→                    f"Total: {epoch_avg['total']:.4f} | "
   217→                    f"LR: {epoch_avg['lr']:.6f}"
   218→                )
   219→
   220→            # Save checkpoint
   221→            if epoch % 25 == 0 or epoch == self.config.epochs:
   222→                ckpt = {
   223→                    "epoch": epoch,
   224→                    "encoder": self.encoder.state_dict(),
   225→                    "mu_head": self.mu_head.state_dict(),
   226→                    "logvar_head": self.logvar_head.state_dict(),
   227→                    "decoder": self.decoder.state_dict(),
   228→                    "optimizer": optimizer.state_dict(),
   229→                    "config": self.config.__dict__,
   230→                }
   231→                path = checkpoint_dir / f"flume_vae_ep{epoch}.pt"
   232→                torch.save(ckpt, path)
   233→                logger.info(f"Checkpoint saved: {path}")
   234→
   235→        return epoch_metrics
   236→
```

     1→"""Dataset classes for FLUME autoencoder training.
     2→
     3→Loads latent trajectories from mass simulation checkpoints
     4→for training the VAE encoder/decoder.
     5→"""
     6→
     7→from __future__ import annotations
     8→
     9→import json
    10→import logging
    11→from pathlib import Path
    12→
    13→import numpy as np
    14→import torch
    15→from torch.utils.data import Dataset
    16→
    17→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    18→
    19→
    20→class FlumeTrajectoryDataset(Dataset):
    21→    """Load mass sim checkpoint trajectories as training data.
    22→
    23→    Each sample is a 256D latent vector from a simulation checkpoint.
    24→    The dataset loads JSONL artifacts or numpy arrays from the mass sim
    25→    output directory.
    26→
    27→    Parameters
    28→    ----------
    29→    data_dir : str or Path
    30→        Directory containing mass sim artifacts.
    31→    max_samples : int
    32→        Maximum number of samples to load (default 100000).
    33→    z_dim : int
    34→        Expected latent dimensionality (default 256).
    35→    """
    36→
    37→    def __init__(
    38→        self,
    39→        data_dir: str | Path = "data/mass_sim/artifacts",
    40→        max_samples: int = 100_000,
    41→        z_dim: int = 256,
    42→    ) -> None:
    43→        self.data_dir = Path(data_dir)
    44→        self.z_dim = z_dim
    45→        self.vectors: list[np.ndarray] = []
    46→
    47→        self._load_data(max_samples)
    48→        logger.info(f"Loaded {len(self.vectors)} trajectory samples from {data_dir}")
    49→
    50→    def _load_data(self, max_samples: int) -> None:
    51→        """Load data from available sources."""
    52→        # Try numpy checkpoints first
    53→        npy_files = sorted(self.data_dir.glob("**/*.npy"))
    54→        for npy_file in npy_files:
    55→            if len(self.vectors) >= max_samples:
    56→                break
    57→            try:
    58→                data = np.load(npy_file)
    59→                if data.ndim == 2 and data.shape[1] == self.z_dim:
    60→                    for row in data:
    61→                        self.vectors.append(row.astype(np.float32))
    62→                        if len(self.vectors) >= max_samples:
    63→                            break
    64→            except Exception as e:
    65→                logger.debug(f"Skipping {npy_file}: {e}")
    66→
    67→        # Fall back to JSONL checkpoints
    68→        if not self.vectors:
    69→            jsonl_files = sorted(self.data_dir.glob("**/*.jsonl"))
    70→            for jsonl_file in jsonl_files:
    71→                if len(self.vectors) >= max_samples:
    72→                    break
    73→                try:
    74→                    with open(jsonl_file) as f:
    75→                        for line in f:
    76→                            record = json.loads(line)
    77→                            if "latent" in record:
    78→                                vec = np.array(record["latent"], dtype=np.float32)
    79→                                if vec.shape == (self.z_dim,):
    80→                                    self.vectors.append(vec)
    81→                except Exception as e:
    82→                    logger.debug(f"Skipping {jsonl_file}: {e}")
    83→
    84→        # Generate synthetic data if nothing loaded (for initial training)
    85→        if not self.vectors:
    86→            logger.warning("No training data found. Generating synthetic samples.")
    87→            rng = np.random.default_rng(42)
    88→            n_synthetic = min(max_samples, 10000)
    89→            for _ in range(n_synthetic):
    90→                # Samples centered near HIHO target with varied spread
    91→                vec = rng.normal(0.5, 0.15, (self.z_dim,)).astype(np.float32)
    92→                self.vectors.append(vec)
    93→
    94→    def __len__(self) -> int:
    95→        return len(self.vectors)
    96→
    97→    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> torch.Tensor:
    98→        return torch.from_numpy(self.vectors[idx])
    99→
   100→
   101→class SyntheticFlumeDataset(Dataset):
   102→    """Generate synthetic FLUME training data on the fly.
   103→
   104→    Creates normally distributed latent vectors centered at HIHO target.
   105→    Useful for bootstrapping training before real sim data is available.
   106→
   107→    Parameters
   108→    ----------
   109→    n_samples : int
   110→        Number of samples (default 10000).
   111→    z_dim : int
   112→        Latent dimension (default 256).
   113→    seed : int
   114→        RNG seed (default 42).
   115→    """
   116→
   117→    def __init__(
   118→        self, n_samples: int = 10000, z_dim: int = 256, seed: int = 42
   119→    ) -> None:
   120→        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
   121→        self.data = rng.normal(0.5, 0.15, (n_samples, z_dim)).astype(np.float32)
   122→
   123→    def __len__(self) -> int:
   124→        return len(self.data)
   125→
   126→    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> torch.Tensor:
   127→        return torch.from_numpy(self.data[idx])
   128→
```

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/flume/experience_encoder.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Encode agentic execution experiences as 256D vectors for FLUME VAE training.\n\n256D encoding scheme:\n  [0:12]   12D axiomatic trajectory (from JourneyTracker)\n  [12:24]  12 scalar execution metrics\n  [24:29]  5 operation type one-hot\n  [29:256] 227 semantic fingerprint (deterministic SHA-256 hash expansion)\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport hashlib\nimport math\n\nimport numpy as np\n\n# Dimension layout\n_TRAJECTORY_DIM = 12  # dims [0:12]\n_METRICS_DIM = 12  # dims [12:24]\n_OP_TYPE_DIM = 5  # dims [24:29]\n_FINGERPRINT_DIM = 227  # dims [29:256]\nTOTAL_DIM = _TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + _OP_TYPE_DIM + _FINGERPRINT_DIM  # 256\n\n# Canonical operation types (matches JourneyTracker.OperationType)\nOPERATION_TYPES = (\"generate\", \"analyze\", \"search\", \"transform\", \"persist\")\n\n# Metric keys in canonical order (dims 12-23)\nMETRIC_KEYS = (\n    \"phi_score\",\n    \"anomaly_score\",\n    \"misalignment_score\",\n    \"intent_confidence\",\n    \"duration_s\",\n    \"tokens_used\",\n    \"cache_hit_rate\",\n    \"success\",\n    \"token_efficiency\",\n    \"quality_score\",\n    \"convergence_trend\",\n    \"cost_usd\",\n)\n\n\nclass ExperienceEncoder:\n    \"\"\"Encode a single execution experience dict into a 256D float32 vector.\n\n    The encoding is fully deterministic: same input dict always produces\n    the same output vector.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def encode(self, experience: dict) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Encode an experience record into a 256D float32 vector.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        experience : dict\n            Must contain at least one of:\n            - ``trajectory``: list/array of 12D floats\n            - ``operation_type``: one of OPERATION_TYPES\n            Additional scalar metrics are pulled by METRIC_KEYS names.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        np.ndarray\n            Shape ``(256,)`` dtype ``float32``.\n        \"\"\"\n        vec = np.zeros(TOTAL_DIM, dtype=np.float32)\n\n        # --- Dims [0:12]: 12D trajectory ---\n        traj = experience.get(\"trajectory\")\n        if traj is not None:\n            traj_arr = np.asarray(traj, dtype=np.float32).ravel()\n            n = min(len(traj_arr), _TRAJECTORY_DIM)\n            vec[:n] = traj_arr[:n]\n\n        # --- Dims [12:24]: scalar execution metrics ---\n        for i, key in enumerate(METRIC_KEYS):\n            raw = experience.get(key, 0.0)\n            val = float(raw) if raw is not None else 0.0\n            # Log-scale normalization for duration and tokens\n            if key == \"duration_s\":\n                val = math.log1p(val) / 10.0  # log(1+s)/10 → ~0-1 for 0-22000s\n            elif key == \"tokens_used\":\n                val = math.log1p(val) / 15.0  # log(1+t)/15 → ~0-1 for 0-3M tokens\n            elif key == \"cost_usd\":\n                val = math.log1p(val * 100) / 10.0  # scale cents then log\n            # Clamp to [0,1] for bounded metrics\n            vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + i] = float(np.clip(val, -1.0, 2.0))\n\n        # --- Dims [24:29]: operation type one-hot ---\n        op_type = experience.get(\"operation_type\", \"\")\n        if isinstance(op_type, str):\n            op_type = op_type.lower()\n        if op_type in OPERATION_TYPES:\n            idx = OPERATION_TYPES.index(op_type)\n            vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + idx] = 1.0\n\n        # --- Dims [29:256]: semantic fingerprint ---\n        fingerprint_text = self._build_fingerprint_text(experience)\n        vec[_TRAJECTORY_DIM + _METRICS_DIM + _OP_TYPE_DIM :] = (\n            self._sha256_expand(fingerprint_text, _FINGERPRINT_DIM)\n        )\n\n        return vec\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _build_fingerprint_text(experience: dict) -> str:\n        \"\"\"Build a stable text key from experience metadata for hashing.\"\"\"\n        parts = []\n        for key in (\"mission_id\", \"agent_id\", \"skill_name\", \"input_preview\"):\n            val = experience.get(key)\n            if val:\n                parts.append(str(val))\n        return \"|\".join(parts) if parts else \"unknown\"\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _sha256_expand(text: str, dim: int) -> np.ndarray:\n        \"\"\"Deterministic hash expansion to ``dim`` floats in [0, 1].\n\n        Same approach as JourneyTracker._text_to_latent: SHA-256 bytes\n        cycled with sine-wave modulation for smooth variation.\n        \"\"\"\n        hash_bytes = hashlib.sha256(text.encode(\"utf-8\")).digest()\n        out = np.zeros(dim, dtype=np.float32)\n        for i in range(dim):\n            byte_val = hash_bytes[i % len(hash_bytes)]\n            phase = (2.0 * math.pi * i) / dim\n            out[i] = (\n                (byte_val / 255.0) * 0.5\n                + 0.25 * math.sin(phase)\n                + 0.25 * math.cos(phase * 2)\n            )\n        return out\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/flume/experience_collector.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Collect execution experiences from Parquet, SurrealDB, and vault sources.\n\nThree tiers, each non-blocking:\n  Tier 1: Parquet journey shards  (data/journeys/shard_*.parquet)\n  Tier 2: SurrealDB mission_journey table\n  Tier 3: Vault experiment JSON files\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nimport numpy as np\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n# Default paths\n_PARQUET_DIR = Path(\"data/journeys\")\n_VAULT_DIR = Path.home() / \"vaults\" / \"cohezion-vault\" / \"experiments\"\n\n\nclass ExperienceCollector:\n    \"\"\"Gather execution experiences from all available data sources.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    parquet_dir : Path\n        Directory containing ``shard_*.parquet`` files.\n    vault_dir : Path\n        Directory containing vault experiment JSON files.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        parquet_dir: Path | str = _PARQUET_DIR,\n        vault_dir: Path | str = _VAULT_DIR,\n    ) -> None:\n        self.parquet_dir = Path(parquet_dir)\n        self.vault_dir = Path(vault_dir)\n\n    def collect_all(self, max_samples: int = 100_000) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Collect experiences from all tiers.\n\n        Returns a list of normalized experience dicts with keys matching\n        the ExperienceEncoder schema.\n        \"\"\"\n        records: list[dict] = []\n        records.extend(self._collect_parquet(max_samples))\n        if len(records) < max_samples:\n            records.extend(self._collect_surreal(max_samples - len(records)))\n        if len(records) < max_samples:\n            records.extend(self._collect_vault(max_samples - len(records)))\n        logger.info(\"Collected %d total experience records\", len(records))\n        return records[:max_samples]\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Tier 1: Parquet shards\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def _collect_parquet(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Read journey shards from Parquet files.\"\"\"\n        if not self.parquet_dir.is_dir():\n            logger.debug(\"Parquet dir %s not found, skipping tier 1\", self.parquet_dir)\n            return []\n        parquet_files = sorted(self.parquet_dir.glob(\"shard_*.parquet\"))\n        if not parquet_files:\n            return []\n\n        try:\n            import pyarrow.parquet as pq\n        except ImportError:\n            logger.debug(\"pyarrow not installed, skipping parquet tier\")\n            return []\n\n        records: list[dict] = []\n        for pf in parquet_files:\n            if len(records) >= max_samples:\n                break\n            try:\n                table = pq.read_table(pf)\n                for row in table.to_pydict().values():\n                    # pydict returns {col: [values]} — need to iterate rows\n                    break  # just need to check structure\n                df_rows = table.to_pandas().to_dict(orient=\"records\")\n                for row in df_rows:\n                    if len(records) >= max_samples:\n                        break\n                    records.append(self._normalize_parquet_row(row))\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Skipping %s: %s\", pf, e)\n        logger.info(\"Tier 1 (parquet): collected %d records\", len(records))\n        return records\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _normalize_parquet_row(row: dict) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Convert a parquet row into the canonical experience schema.\"\"\"\n        # Extract the last 12D trajectory point (or average if multiple)\n        trajectory = np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32)\n        state_traj = row.get(\"state_trajectory\")\n        if state_traj is not None and len(state_traj) > 0:\n            # state_trajectory is list of 12D arrays — take last point\n            last = state_traj[-1]\n            arr = np.asarray(last, dtype=np.float32).ravel()\n            n = min(len(arr), 12)\n            trajectory[:n] = arr[:n]\n\n        return {\n            \"trajectory\": trajectory,\n            \"mission_id\": row.get(\"mission_id\", \"\"),\n            \"agent_id\": row.get(\"agent_id\", \"\"),\n            \"skill_name\": row.get(\"skill_name\", \"\"),\n            \"input_preview\": row.get(\"input_preview\", \"\"),\n            \"phi_score\": float(row.get(\"phi_score\", 0.0)),\n            \"novelty\": float(row.get(\"novelty\", 0.0)),\n            \"operation_type\": \"generate\",  # default for journeys\n        }\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Tier 2: SurrealDB mission_journey\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def _collect_surreal(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Query SurrealDB for mission journey records (non-blocking).\"\"\"\n        try:\n            import asyncio\n\n            return asyncio.get_event_loop().run_until_complete(\n                self._collect_surreal_async(max_samples)\n            )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"SurrealDB tier skipped: %s\", e)\n            return []\n\n    async def _collect_surreal_async(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Async SurrealDB collection.\"\"\"\n        try:\n            from cohezion.core.persistence.surreal_client import SurrealClient\n\n            client = SurrealClient()\n            await client.connect()\n            result = await client.query(\n                f\"SELECT * FROM mission_journey LIMIT {max_samples}\"\n            )\n            records = []\n            if result and isinstance(result, list):\n                for row in result:\n                    if isinstance(row, dict):\n                        records.append(self._normalize_surreal_row(row))\n            logger.info(\"Tier 2 (surreal): collected %d records\", len(records))\n            return records\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"SurrealDB query failed: %s\", e)\n            return []\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _normalize_surreal_row(row: dict) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a SurrealDB mission_journey record.\"\"\"\n        trajectory = np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32)\n        state_traj = row.get(\"state_trajectory\")\n        if state_traj is not None and len(state_traj) > 0:\n            last = state_traj[-1] if isinstance(state_traj[-1], (list, np.ndarray)) else state_traj\n            arr = np.asarray(last, dtype=np.float32).ravel()\n            n = min(len(arr), 12)\n            trajectory[:n] = arr[:n]\n\n        return {\n            \"trajectory\": trajectory,\n            \"mission_id\": row.get(\"id\", row.get(\"mission_id\", \"\")),\n            \"agent_id\": row.get(\"agent_id\", \"\"),\n            \"skill_name\": row.get(\"skill_name\", \"\"),\n            \"input_preview\": \"\",\n            \"phi_score\": float(row.get(\"phi_score\", 0.0)),\n            \"operation_type\": row.get(\"operation_type\", \"generate\"),\n        }\n\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    # Tier 3: Vault experiment JSONs\n    # ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    def _collect_vault(self, max_samples: int) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Walk vault experiment files for experience data.\"\"\"\n        if not self.vault_dir.is_dir():\n            logger.debug(\"Vault dir %s not found, skipping tier 3\", self.vault_dir)\n            return []\n\n        records: list[dict] = []\n        for json_path in sorted(self.vault_dir.rglob(\"*.json\")):\n            if len(records) >= max_samples:\n                break\n            try:\n                with open(json_path) as f:\n                    data = json.load(f)\n                if isinstance(data, dict):\n                    records.append(self._normalize_vault_record(data, json_path.stem))\n                elif isinstance(data, list):\n                    for item in data:\n                        if isinstance(item, dict) and len(records) < max_samples:\n                            records.append(self._normalize_vault_record(item, json_path.stem))\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Skipping vault file %s: %s\", json_path, e)\n\n        logger.info(\"Tier 3 (vault): collected %d records\", len(records))\n        return records\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _normalize_vault_record(record: dict, source: str) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"Normalize a vault experiment JSON into the canonical schema.\"\"\"\n        return {\n            \"trajectory\": np.zeros(12, dtype=np.float32),\n            \"mission_id\": record.get(\"id\", source),\n            \"agent_id\": record.get(\"agent_id\", \"\"),\n            \"skill_name\": record.get(\"skill_name\", record.get(\"title\", \"\")),\n            \"input_preview\": record.get(\"hypothesis\", record.get(\"description\", \"\")),\n            \"phi_score\": float(record.get(\"phi_score\", 0.0)),\n            \"operation_type\": record.get(\"operation_type\", \"analyze\"),\n        }\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/flume/experience_dataset.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Torch Dataset that encodes execution experiences as 256D vectors.\n\nCompatible with ``FlumeVAETrainer.train(dataset=...)`` — each sample\nis a ``torch.Tensor`` of shape ``(256,)``.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport torch\nfrom torch.utils.data import Dataset\n\nfrom cohezion.flume.experience_encoder import TOTAL_DIM, ExperienceEncoder\n\n\nclass ExperienceDataset(Dataset):\n    \"\"\"Pre-encoded experience dataset for FLUME VAE training.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    experiences : list[dict]\n        Raw experience dicts (see ExperienceEncoder for schema).\n    seed : int\n        RNG seed for reproducibility.\n    augment : bool\n        If True, add small gaussian noise for regularization.\n    augment_std : float\n        Standard deviation of augmentation noise.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        experiences: list[dict],\n        seed: int = 42,\n        augment: bool = False,\n        augment_std: float = 0.01,\n    ) -> None:\n        self._encoder = ExperienceEncoder()\n        self._augment = augment\n        self._augment_std = augment_std\n        self._rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n\n        # Pre-encode all experiences at init for fast __getitem__\n        self._vectors = np.stack(\n            [self._encoder.encode(exp) for exp in experiences]\n        ) if experiences else np.empty((0, TOTAL_DIM), dtype=np.float32)\n\n    def __len__(self) -> int:\n        return len(self._vectors)\n\n    def __getitem__(self, idx: int) -> torch.Tensor:\n        vec = self._vectors[idx].copy()\n        if self._augment:\n            noise = self._rng.normal(0.0, self._augment_std, vec.shape).astype(\n                np.float32\n            )\n            vec = vec + noise\n        return torch.from_numpy(vec)\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/flume/experience_pipeline.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"End-to-end pipeline: collect experiences -> encode -> train FLUME VAE.\n\nOrchestrates ExperienceCollector, ExperienceDataset, and FlumeVAETrainer\nwith graceful synthetic fallback when real data is insufficient.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\nfrom cohezion.flume.experience_dataset import ExperienceDataset\nfrom cohezion.flume.training import FlumeVAETrainer, TrainConfig\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n\nclass ExperienceTrainingPipeline:\n    \"\"\"Collect real experiences, encode as 256D, and train the FLUME VAE.\n\n    Parameters\n    ----------\n    collector : ExperienceCollector or None\n        Custom collector; uses default if None.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    def __init__(self, collector: ExperienceCollector | None = None) -> None:\n        self.collector = collector or ExperienceCollector()\n\n    async def run(\n        self,\n        min_real: int = 10,\n        max_samples: int = 10_000,\n        epochs: int = 50,\n        batch_size: int = 64,\n        lr: float = 1e-3,\n        seed: int = 42,\n        synthetic_fallback: bool = True,\n        checkpoint_dir: str = \"data/flume/checkpoints\",\n    ) -> Path:\n        \"\"\"Run the full experience -> VAE training pipeline.\n\n        Parameters\n        ----------\n        min_real : int\n            Minimum real experiences required. If fewer are found and\n            ``synthetic_fallback`` is True, pad with synthetic data.\n        max_samples : int\n            Maximum total samples for training.\n        epochs : int\n            Training epochs.\n        batch_size : int\n            Batch size for DataLoader.\n        lr : float\n            Learning rate.\n        seed : int\n            RNG seed for reproducibility.\n        synthetic_fallback : bool\n            If True and real data < min_real, pad with synthetic data.\n        checkpoint_dir : str\n            Directory for saving model checkpoints.\n\n        Returns\n        -------\n        Path\n            Path to the final checkpoint file.\n        \"\"\"\n        # --- Step 1: Collect real experiences ---\n        logger.info(\"Collecting real experiences...\")\n        experiences = self.collector.collect_all(max_samples=max_samples)\n        n_real = len(experiences)\n        logger.info(\"Found %d real experience records\", n_real)\n\n        # --- Step 2: Synthetic fallback if needed ---\n        if n_real < min_real and synthetic_fallback:\n            n_synthetic = max_samples - n_real\n            logger.info(\n                \"Real data (%d) < min_real (%d), padding with %d synthetic samples\",\n                n_real,\n                min_real,\n                n_synthetic,\n            )\n            experiences.extend(self._generate_synthetic(n_synthetic, seed))\n        elif n_real < min_real and not synthetic_fallback:\n            msg = (\n                f\"Only {n_real} real experiences found (need {min_real}). \"\n                \"Set synthetic_fallback=True to pad with synthetic data.\"\n            )\n            raise ValueError(msg)\n\n        # --- Step 3: Build dataset ---\n        dataset = ExperienceDataset(experiences, seed=seed)\n        logger.info(\"Dataset ready: %d samples\", len(dataset))\n\n        # --- Step 4: Train ---\n        config = TrainConfig(\n            z_dim=256,\n            batch_size=min(batch_size, len(dataset)),\n            epochs=epochs,\n            lr=lr,\n            checkpoint_dir=checkpoint_dir,\n        )\n        trainer = FlumeVAETrainer(config)\n        metrics = trainer.train(dataset=dataset)\n\n        # --- Step 5: Report ---\n        final_epoch = metrics[-1] if metrics else {}\n        logger.info(\n            \"Training complete. Final loss: %.4f | Real samples: %d | Total: %d\",\n            final_epoch.get(\"total\", float(\"nan\")),\n            n_real,\n            len(dataset),\n        )\n\n        checkpoint_path = Path(checkpoint_dir) / f\"flume_vae_ep{epochs}.pt\"\n        return checkpoint_path\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _generate_synthetic(count: int, seed: int) -> list[dict]:\n        \"\"\"Generate synthetic experience dicts with gaussian noise.\"\"\"\n        import numpy as np\n\n        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)\n        op_types = (\"generate\", \"analyze\", \"search\", \"transform\", \"persist\")\n        records = []\n        for i in range(count):\n            records.append(\n                {\n                    \"trajectory\": rng.normal(0.5, 0.15, 12).astype(np.float32),\n                    \"mission_id\": f\"synthetic_{i}\",\n                    \"agent_id\": \"synthetic\",\n                    \"skill_name\": \"synthetic\",\n                    \"operation_type\": op_types[i % len(op_types)],\n                    \"phi_score\": float(rng.uniform(0.3, 0.95)),\n                    \"cache_hit_rate\": float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0)),\n                    \"success\": float(rng.choice([0.0, 1.0])),\n                }\n            )\n        return records\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"CLI driver: train the FLUME VAE on real agentic experiences.\n\nUsage:\n    uv run python scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py --epochs 5 --min-real 1\n    uv run python scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py --no-synthetic --min-real 50\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport argparse\nimport asyncio\nimport logging\nimport sys\n\n\ndef main() -> None:\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Train FLUME VAE on collected execution experiences.\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--min-real\",\n        type=int,\n        default=10,\n        help=\"Minimum real experience records required (default: 10)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--max-samples\",\n        type=int,\n        default=10_000,\n        help=\"Maximum total training samples (default: 10000)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--epochs\", type=int, default=50, help=\"Training epochs (default: 50)\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--batch-size\", type=int, default=64, help=\"Batch size (default: 64)\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--lr\", type=float, default=1e-3, help=\"Learning rate (default: 0.001)\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--seed\", type=int, default=42, help=\"Random seed (default: 42)\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--session\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"\",\n        help=\"Session tag for checkpoint naming (optional)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--no-synthetic\",\n        action=\"store_true\",\n        help=\"Disable synthetic data fallback — fail if insufficient real data\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--checkpoint-dir\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"data/flume/checkpoints\",\n        help=\"Checkpoint output directory (default: data/flume/checkpoints)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"-v\", \"--verbose\", action=\"store_true\", help=\"Enable debug logging\"\n    )\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    logging.basicConfig(\n        level=logging.DEBUG if args.verbose else logging.INFO,\n        format=\"%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(name)s: %(message)s\",\n    )\n\n    from cohezion.flume.experience_pipeline import ExperienceTrainingPipeline\n\n    pipeline = ExperienceTrainingPipeline()\n    try:\n        checkpoint = asyncio.run(\n            pipeline.run(\n                min_real=args.min_real,\n                max_samples=args.max_samples,\n                epochs=args.epochs,\n                batch_size=args.batch_size,\n                lr=args.lr,\n                seed=args.seed,\n                synthetic_fallback=not args.no_synthetic,\n                checkpoint_dir=args.checkpoint_dir,\n            )\n        )\n        print(f\"\\nCheckpoint saved: {checkpoint}\")\n    except ValueError as e:\n        print(f\"Error: {e}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        sys.exit(1)\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Tests for the Experience -> VAE Training Pipeline.\n\n5 focused tests covering encoder, dataset, collector, and end-to-end pipeline.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nfrom pathlib import Path\nfrom unittest.mock import patch\n\nimport numpy as np\nimport pytest\nimport torch\n\nfrom cohezion.flume.experience_encoder import (\n    OPERATION_TYPES,\n    TOTAL_DIM,\n    ExperienceEncoder,\n)\n\n\ndef _make_experience(**overrides: object) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Create a minimal valid experience dict.\"\"\"\n    base: dict = {\n        \"trajectory\": np.random.default_rng(42).normal(0.5, 0.15, 12).astype(np.float32),\n        \"mission_id\": \"test-mission-1\",\n        \"agent_id\": \"test-agent\",\n        \"skill_name\": \"research\",\n        \"input_preview\": \"test input\",\n        \"operation_type\": \"generate\",\n        \"phi_score\": 0.85,\n    }\n    base.update(overrides)\n    return base\n\n\nclass TestExperienceEncoder:\n    \"\"\"Test ExperienceEncoder output shape, determinism, and one-hot encoding.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_encoder_output_shape_and_determinism(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Encode same experience twice -> identical 256D vectors.\"\"\"\n        encoder = ExperienceEncoder()\n        exp = _make_experience()\n\n        vec1 = encoder.encode(exp)\n        vec2 = encoder.encode(exp)\n\n        assert vec1.shape == (TOTAL_DIM,)\n        assert vec1.dtype == np.float32\n        np.testing.assert_array_equal(vec1, vec2)\n\n    def test_encoder_operation_type_onehot(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Each operation type activates exactly the correct dimension.\"\"\"\n        encoder = ExperienceEncoder()\n\n        for i, op in enumerate(OPERATION_TYPES):\n            exp = _make_experience(operation_type=op)\n            vec = encoder.encode(exp)\n\n            # One-hot region is dims [24:29]\n            onehot = vec[24:29]\n            assert onehot[i] == 1.0, f\"Expected dim {i} active for {op}\"\n            # All others should be 0\n            for j in range(len(OPERATION_TYPES)):\n                if j != i:\n                    assert onehot[j] == 0.0, f\"Expected dim {j} inactive for {op}\"\n\n\nclass TestExperienceDataset:\n    \"\"\"Test ExperienceDataset returns correct tensors.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_dataset_returns_correct_tensor(self) -> None:\n        \"\"\"dataset[0] is a torch.Tensor of shape (256,).\"\"\"\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_dataset import ExperienceDataset\n\n        experiences = [_make_experience(mission_id=f\"m{i}\") for i in range(5)]\n        ds = ExperienceDataset(experiences, seed=42)\n\n        assert len(ds) == 5\n        sample = ds[0]\n        assert isinstance(sample, torch.Tensor)\n        assert sample.shape == (TOTAL_DIM,)\n        assert sample.dtype == torch.float32\n\n\nclass TestExperienceCollector:\n    \"\"\"Test ExperienceCollector handles missing directories gracefully.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_collector_handles_missing_dirs(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Empty/missing parquet and vault dirs -> empty list, no crash.\"\"\"\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\n\n        collector = ExperienceCollector(\n            parquet_dir=tmp_path / \"nonexistent_parquet\",\n            vault_dir=tmp_path / \"nonexistent_vault\",\n        )\n        results = collector.collect_all()\n        assert isinstance(results, list)\n        assert len(results) == 0\n\n\nclass TestExperiencePipeline:\n    \"\"\"Test end-to-end pipeline with synthetic fallback.\"\"\"\n\n    def test_pipeline_trains_with_synthetic_fallback(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Full pipeline with no real data -> checkpoint saved via synthetic fallback.\"\"\"\n        import asyncio\n\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_collector import ExperienceCollector\n        from cohezion.flume.experience_pipeline import ExperienceTrainingPipeline\n\n        checkpoint_dir = tmp_path / \"checkpoints\"\n        collector = ExperienceCollector(\n            parquet_dir=tmp_path / \"empty_parquet\",\n            vault_dir=tmp_path / \"empty_vault\",\n        )\n        pipeline = ExperienceTrainingPipeline(collector=collector)\n\n        epochs = 2\n        checkpoint_path = asyncio.run(\n            pipeline.run(\n                min_real=1,\n                max_samples=200,\n                epochs=epochs,\n                batch_size=32,\n                lr=1e-3,\n                seed=42,\n                synthetic_fallback=True,\n                checkpoint_dir=str(checkpoint_dir),\n            )\n        )\n\n        # Checkpoint should exist\n        assert checkpoint_path.exists(), f\"Checkpoint not found at {checkpoint_path}\"\n        # Verify it's a valid torch checkpoint\n        ckpt = torch.load(checkpoint_path, weights_only=False)\n        assert \"epoch\" in ckpt\n        assert ckpt[\"epoch\"] == epochs\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "REDACTED.md",
  "content": "# EXPERIENCE_VAE_TRAINING_PRIME\n\n## Intent\nTrain the FLUME VAE on real agentic execution experiences instead of synthetic gaussian noise, closing the feedback loop between compound execution and universe simulation.\n\n## Trigger\n- After accumulating >=10 real execution records in Parquet/SurrealDB/vault\n- After a session with significant compound execution activity\n- Periodically (weekly) to update the VAE on latest behavior distributions\n\n## Procedure\n\n### 1. Collect Experiences\nGather execution records from three tiers:\n- **Tier 1**: `data/journeys/shard_*.parquet` — 12D trajectories + metadata\n- **Tier 2**: SurrealDB `mission_journey` table (non-blocking, skip if unavailable)\n- **Tier 3**: `~/vaults/cohezion-vault/experiments/**/*.json` vault files\n\n### 2. Encode as 256D Vectors\nEach experience is encoded via `ExperienceEncoder`:\n```\nDims [0:12]   — 12D axiomatic trajectory\nDims [12:24]  — 12 scalar execution metrics (phi, anomaly, tokens, cost...)\nDims [24:29]  — 5 operation type one-hot (generate/analyze/search/transform/persist)\nDims [29:256] — 227 semantic fingerprint (SHA-256 hash expansion)\n```\n\n### 3. Build Dataset\n- Pre-encode all experiences at init (fast `__getitem__`)\n- Optional gaussian noise augmentation for regularization\n- Compatible with existing `FlumeVAETrainer.train(dataset=...)`\n\n### 4. Train VAE\n- Uses `TrainConfig(z_dim=256)` and `FlumeVAETrainer`\n- Graceful fallback: if real data < threshold, pad with `SyntheticFlumeDataset`\n- Checkpoints saved to `data/flume/checkpoints/`\n\n### 5. Verify\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py -v\nuv run python scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py --epochs 5 --min-real 1\n```\n\n## Key Files\n| File | Purpose |\n|------|---------|\n| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_encoder.py` | 256D encoding (trajectory + metrics + one-hot + fingerprint) |\n| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_collector.py` | Multi-tier data collection (Parquet, SurrealDB, vault) |\n| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_dataset.py` | Torch Dataset returning 256D tensors |\n| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_pipeline.py` | End-to-end orchestration with synthetic fallback |\n| `scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py` | CLI entry point |\n\n## Success Criteria\n- Encoder output is deterministic (same input → same 256D vector)\n- Pipeline completes with synthetic fallback when no real data exists\n- Checkpoint is valid and loadable\n- 5 tests pass in `tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py`\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n- Training on only synthetic data without logging a warning\n- Blocking on SurrealDB when it's unavailable\n- Using Python `hash()` instead of SHA-256 (non-deterministic across sessions)\n- Hardcoding operation types instead of matching JourneyTracker.OperationType\n\n## Tags\n`flume`, `vae`, `training`, `experience`, `compound-engineering`\n"
}

| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_collector.py` | `ExperienceCollector` — gathers experiences from 3 tiers: Parquet shards, SurrealDB `mission_journey`, and vault experiment JSONs |
| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_dataset.py` | `ExperienceDataset(Dataset)` — torch Dataset returning `(256,)` tensors, pre-encodes at init for fast `__getitem__` |
| `src/cohezion/flume/experience_pipeline.py` | `ExperienceTrainingPipeline` — orchestrates collect → encode → train → checkpoint with synthetic fallback |
| `scripts/drivers/train_experience_vae.py` | CLI entry point with argparse (`--epochs`, `--min-real`, `--no-synthetic`, etc.) |
| `src/cohezion/skills/EXPERIENCE_VAE_TRAINING_PRIME.md` | PRIME skill definition for the full pipeline pattern |
| `tests/flume/test_experience_pipeline.py` | 5 focused tests — all passing |

{
  "project": "cohezion",
  "title": "Experience → VAE Training Pipeline (Session 58)",
  "context": "FLUME VAE trained only on synthetic gaussian noise (0.5 ± 0.15). Meanwhile, compound executor captures rich 12D trajectories, coherence metrics, and anomaly scores persisted to Parquet, SurrealDB, and vault — but none fed back into the VAE.",
  "decision": "Built a 4-stage pipeline: ExperienceCollector (3-tier: Parquet/SurrealDB/vault) → ExperienceEncoder (256D: 12D trajectory + 12 metrics + 5 one-hot + 227 SHA-256 fingerprint) → ExperienceDataset (torch Dataset) → FlumeVAETrainer with synthetic fallback. CLI driver and PRIME skill definition included.",
  "rationale": "Closes the feedback loop so the VAE learns from actual agentic behavior distributions instead of random noise. Preserves full 256D compatibility with existing VAE architecture. Graceful degradation (synthetic fallback) means pipeline works even with minimal real data. Non-blocking SurrealDB ensures no dependency on DB availability.",
  "alternatives_considered": "1. Train on 12D only (simpler but loses metric/semantic context). 2. Use sentence-transformer embeddings instead of SHA-256 (heavier, non-deterministic). 3. Stream training from live executor (complex, premature — need batch pipeline first)."
}

and quantization options:\n\n## Available Tags and Sizes\n\nGLM-4.7-Flash is available in three quantization variants through Ollama:\n- **q4_K_M**: 19GB\n- **q8_0**: 32GB  \n- **bf16**: 60GB\n\nAll versions have a 198K context window for text input.\n\n## Model Details\n\nGLM-4.7-Flash is a 30B-A3B MoE model, and this model requires Ollama 0.14.3 which is currently in pre-release.\n\n## Quantization Comparison\n\nFor reference on these quantization levels:\n- Q8 uses approximately 22GB of VRAM for excellent quality at 2x speed, while Q4 uses approximately 15GB of VRAM with good quality at 3x speed\n- With 4-bit quantization, the model can be squeezed into 15-20GB, making it runnable on high-end consumer cards like an RTX 3090 or 4090\n\nThe q4_K_M variant offers the best balance of file size and quality for most users, while q8_0 provides higher quality at the cost of more storage/VRAM, and bf16 represents the full precision version for maximum quality.\n\n\nREMINDER: A Practical Guide for AI Enthusiasts\",\"url\":\"https://onedollarvps.com/blogs/how-to-run-qwen3-locally\"},{\"title\":\"unsloth/Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct-GGUF · Hugging Face\",\"url\":\"https://huggingface.co/unsloth/Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B-Instruct-GGUF\"},{\"title\":\"Qwen3-30B-A3B: Specifications and GPU VRAM ...\",\"url\":\"https://apxml.com/models/qwen3-30b-a3b\"}]\n\nBased on the search results, here's detailed information about the Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B model for Ollama:\n\n## Download Size\n\nThe Q4_K_M quantization version is 19GB, making it one of the more accessible quantized variants for download and local storage.\n\n## RAM Requirements\n\nRAM requirements vary significantly based on quantization level:\n\n- **System RAM**: At least 32GB of fast DDR4 or DDR5 RAM is recommended, with users reporting speeds between 12-15 tokens per second with a 4-bit quantized model on modern CPUs.\n\n- **For optimal performance**: To achieve inference speeds of 6+ tokens per second, available memory should match or exceed the size of the model; for example, a 30GB 1-bit quantized model requires at least 150GB of memory, while the Q2_K_XL quant (180GB) requires at least 180GB of unified memory.\n\n- **For GPU acceleration**: The UD-Q4_K_XL quantized model with defaults consumes roughly 32GB of VRAM.\n\n## Quantization Options\n\nStart with the highest-bit quantization that fits in your VRAM, using GGUF for llama.cpp or MLX for Apple Silicon. Different quantization levels are available, with Q4_K_M being a popular balanced option at 19GB.\n\n## Key Model Features\n\nThe model offers 30B total parameters with only 3.3B activated, delivering strong performance while maintaining efficiency.\n\n\nREMINDER: You MUST include the sources above in your response to the user using markdown hyperlinks."}]},"uuid":"85cd1077-3451-466f-a3d1-bd0336586075","timestamp":"2026-02-13T19:17:24.224Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"8a0b27bc-135c-47c9-9099-fc11f24332db"}

| 3.3 | Integrate VAE latent space with `SkillSelector` — use proximity in 256D space for skill matching | 2,000 | 3.2 |
| 3.4 | Add coherence prediction from VAE — estimate task difficulty before execution | 1,500 | 3.2 |
| 3.5 | Build feedback dashboard (Marimo) showing latent space clusters + model performance | 2,000 | 3.2, 2.5 |

### Phase 4: Autonomous Local Agent Swarm (Sessions 68-75, ~3 weeks)

**Goal**: Multi-agent orchestration using local models with self-improving skills.

| Step | Task | Tokens | Deps |
|------|------|--------|------|
| 4.1 | Build `LocalAgentSpawner` — spawn Ollama-backed agents with model-specific configs | 2,000 | 2.2 |
| 4.2 | Implement EDL routing through local models (Architect=glm-4.7-flash, Engineer=qwen3-coder, etc.) | 2,500 | 4.1 |
| 4.3 | Add `SkillConsensusVoter` using local model ensemble (3 models vote on skill updates) | 2,000 | 4.2 |
| 4.4 | Implement autonomous compound loop: execute → collect experience → retrain VAE → refine skills | 3,000 | 3.3, 4.3 |
| 4.5 | Add `RetrospectionEngine` auto-trigger — detect skill degradation, auto-refine | 2,000 | 4.4 |
| 4.6 | Build overnight autonomous improvement driver | 2,000 | 4.5 |

### Phase 5: Universe Simulation with Real Data (Sessions 76-80, ~2 weeks)

**Goal**: 12D universe simulation uses actual experience distributions.

| Step | Task | Tokens | Deps |
|------|------|--------|------|
| 5.1 | Replace synthetic universe initialization with VAE-generated experience vectors | 1,500 | 3.2 |
| 5.2 | Implement divergence detection using real vs generated experience distributions | 2,000 | 5.1 |
| 5.3 | Add HIHO stability monitoring using experience-trained coherence predictor | 1,500 | 3.4 |
| 5.4 | Build full-loop demo: user request → agent swarm → journey tracking → VAE training → simulation | 3,000 | All |

### Timeline Summary

```
Week 1  (Feb 13-19):  Phase 1 — Foundation + model installs + data accumulation
Week 2  (Feb 20-26):  Phase 2 — Intelligent routing + A/B testing
Week 3-4 (Feb 27-Mar 12): Phase 3 — Experience-guided execution
Week 5-7 (Mar 13-Apr 2):  Phase 4 — Autonomous local agent swarm
Week 8-9 (Apr 3-16):      Phase 5 — Universe simulation with real data
```

### Cost Profile
- **Local model inference**: $0.00 (all Ollama, 128GB RAM)
- **Cloud API (Claude)**: Session orchestration only (~10K tokens/session for planning + code review)
- **Total estimated tokens for full plan**: ~40K implementation tokens across ~20 sessions
- **Storage**: ~250GB Ollama models + ~500MB training data + ~50MB Parquet journeys

### Key Risk: Data Sparsity
Currently only 5 real experience records. The entire plan hinges on Phase 1.5-1.6 (instrumenting the compound executor). Without 50+ records by Phase 3, the VAE will produce low-quality latent spaces. Mitigation: synthetic augmentation + noise injection (already built into ExperienceDataset).

  "prompt": "Research how to optimally orchestrate multiple local LLM agents via Ollama on a system with these constraints:\n\n**Hardware:**\n- AMD Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 (16C/32T, AVX-512)\n- 128 GiB LPDDR5X unified RAM (shared between CPU and iGPU)\n- Radeon 8060S iGPU (RDNA 3.5, ROCm unstable — prefer CPU inference)\n- 2TB NVMe storage\n- Global concurrency limit: 4 models simultaneously\n\n**Models available (after current downloads):**\n- glm-4.7-flash (19GB, 30B MoE/3B active) — reasoning + agentic\n- qwen3-coder:30b (18GB, 30B MoE/3B active) — coding\n- gpt-oss:20b (13GB, 21B MoE/3.6B active) — general\n- deepcoder:14b (9GB, 14B dense) — coding\n- phi4-mini-reasoning (~2.4GB, 3.8B dense) — fast reasoning\n- nemotron-3-nano (~24GB, 31.6B MoE/3.2B active) — 1M context\n- snowflake-arctic-embed2 (~670MB) — embeddings\n- nomic-embed-text (274MB) — embeddings\n\n**I need to understand:**\n\n1. **Ollama model loading/unloading behavior** — How does Ollama manage memory when multiple models are loaded? Does it keep models in RAM? How long before it unloads idle models? Can you control `OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE`? What's the cold-start penalty for loading a 19GB model?\n\n2. **Optimal concurrent model combinations** — Given 128GB RAM, what combinations of models can run simultaneously without swapping? Calculate actual memory requirements (model size + KV cache + overhead).\n\n3. **MoE vs Dense inference characteristics** — How do MoE models (3B active from 30B total) compare to dense models on CPU inference speed? Does MoE still need to load all 30B params into RAM even though only 3B activate?\n\n4. **Ollama API features for orchestration** — Does Ollama support:\n   - Batch requests to same model?\n   - Priority queuing?\n   - Model preloading/warming?\n   - Structured output / JSON mode?\n   - Function calling / tool use format?\n   - Keep-alive control per request?\n\n5. **Best practices for multi-agent local orchestration patterns** — Search for how people are building multi-agent systems with Ollama in 2026. Look for:\n   - Agent-to-model assignment strategies\n   - Hot/cold model rotation patterns\n   - Context window management across agents\n   - Token throughput optimization\n\nReturn structured findings with specific numbers where possible (tokens/sec, RAM usage, latency).",
