   601→    def _tune_thresholds_based_on_success(self) -> None:
   602→        """Dynamically tune cost/latency thresholds based on success patterns.
   603→
   604→        If phi3 has high success rate, gradually relax thresholds to route more queries to it.
   605→        """
   606→        # Calculate success rates
   607→        phi3_total = self._phi3_success_count
   608→        qwen_total = self._qwen_success_count
   609→        total_tracked = phi3_total + qwen_total
   610→
   611→        if total_tracked < 10:  # Need minimum sample size
   612→            return
   613→
   614→        phi3_rate = phi3_total / total_tracked if total_tracked > 0 else 0.0
   615→
   616→        # If phi3 has >85% success rate, we can be more aggressive
   617→        if phi3_rate >= 0.85:
   618→            # Increase cost threshold (more aggressive cost cutting)
   619→            if self.cost_threshold < 0.25:
   620→                self.cost_threshold = min(0.25, self.cost_threshold + 0.01)
   621→            # Increase latency threshold tolerance
   622→            if self.latency_threshold < 250.0:
   623→                self.latency_threshold = min(250.0, self.latency_threshold + 5.0)
   624→
   625→        # If phi3 success rate is too low (<60%), be more conservative
   626→        elif phi3_rate < 0.60:
   627→            # Reduce cost threshold
   628→            if self.cost_threshold > 0.05:
   629→                self.cost_threshold = max(0.05, self.cost_threshold - 0.01)
   630→            # Reduce latency threshold tolerance
   631→            if self.latency_threshold > 100.0:
   632→                self.latency_threshold = max(100.0, self.latency_threshold - 5.0)
   633→
   634→    def get_statistics(self) -> RoutingStatistics:
   635→        """Get routing statistics.
   636→
   637→        Returns:
   638→            RoutingStatistics with aggregated metrics
   639→        """
   640→        total = len(self.routing_decisions)
   641→        if total == 0:
   642→            total = 1  # Avoid division by zero
   643→
   644→        simple_count = sum(
   645→            1 for d in self.routing_decisions if d.complexity == QueryComplexity.SIMPLE
   646→        )
   647→        medium_count = sum(
   648→            1 for d in self.routing_decisions if d.complexity == QueryComplexity.MEDIUM
   649→        )
   650→        complex_count = sum(
   651→            1 for d in self.routing_decisions if d.complexity == QueryComplexity.COMPLEX
   652→        )
   653→
   654→        phi3_routed = self.query_count_per_model.get("phi3:mini", 0)
   655→        qwen_routed = self.query_count_per_model.get("qwen3-coder:30b", 0)
   656→        deepseek_routed = self.query_count_per_model.get("deepseek-r1:70b", 0)
   657→
   658→        total_cost = sum(self.cost_per_model.values())
   659→
   660→        # Calculate cost comparison (hypothetical: all queries with deepseek)
   661→        deepseek_only_cost = sum(d.estimated_tokens for d in self.routing_decisions) / 1000.0 * self.MODEL_COSTS[
   662→            "deepseek-r1:8b"
   663→        ]
   664→
   665→        cost_improvement = 0.0
   666→        if deepseek_only_cost > 0:
   667→            cost_improvement = ((deepseek_only_cost - total_cost) / deepseek_only_cost) * 100
   668→
   669→        return RoutingStatistics(
   670→            total_queries=total,
   671→            simple_count=simple_count,
   672→            medium_count=medium_count,
   673→            complex_count=complex_count,
   674→            phi3_routed=phi3_routed,
   675→            qwen_routed=qwen_routed,
   676→            deepseek_routed=deepseek_routed,
   677→            total_cost_usd=total_cost,
   678→            avg_cost_per_query=total_cost / total if total > 0 else 0.0,
   679→            cost_vs_deepseek_only=cost_improvement,
   680→        )
   681→

   684→        self.routing_decisions.clear()

   686→        self.query_count_per_model = {m: 0 for m in self.MODEL_COSTS.keys()}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/scripts/ollama_env.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Cohezion Ollama Environment Configuration\n# Session 59: 3-Tier Hot/Warm/Cold Model Rotation\n#\n# Source this file before starting Ollama, or add to systemd override:\n#   sudo systemctl edit ollama\n#   [Service]\n#   Environment=\"OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=4\"\n#   Environment=\"OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2\"\n#   ...\n\n# Max concurrent models in memory (Tier 1 + Tier 2 = 4 slots)\nexport OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS=4\n\n# Parallel requests per model (2x KV cache per model, acceptable with Q8_0)\nexport OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL=2\n\n# Keep models loaded 30 min after last use (warm tier default)\nexport OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE=\"30m\"\n\n# Enable flash attention (required for KV cache quantization)\nexport OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION=1\n\n# Quantize KV cache to Q8_0 (halves KV memory, negligible quality loss)\nexport OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE=q8_0\n\necho \"Ollama environment configured:\"\necho \"  MAX_LOADED_MODELS=$OLLAMA_MAX_LOADED_MODELS\"\necho \"  NUM_PARALLEL=$OLLAMA_NUM_PARALLEL\"\necho \"  KEEP_ALIVE=$OLLAMA_KEEP_ALIVE\"\necho \"  FLASH_ATTENTION=$OLLAMA_FLASH_ATTENTION\"\necho \"  KV_CACHE_TYPE=$OLLAMA_KV_CACHE_TYPE\"\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/scripts/preload_models.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Cohezion Model Preloader — 3-Tier Hot/Warm/Cold Rotation\n# Session 59: Preload Tier 1 (always hot) and Tier 2 (warm) models\n#\n# Usage: ./scripts/preload_models.sh [--tier1-only]\n#\n# Tier 1 (~5 GB): phi4-mini-reasoning + nomic-embed-text (always hot)\n# Tier 2 (~43 GB): glm-4.7-flash + qwen3-coder:30b (warm, 30min keepalive)\n# Tier 3 (on-demand): gpt-oss:20b, deepcoder:14b, nemotron-3-nano\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nOLLAMA_URL=\"${OLLAMA_BASE_URL:-http://localhost:11434}\"\n\npreload() {\n    local model=\"$1\"\n    local keep_alive=\"$2\"\n    echo \"  Loading $model (keep_alive=$keep_alive)...\"\n    curl -sf \"$OLLAMA_URL/api/generate\" \\\n        -d \"{\\\"model\\\": \\\"$model\\\", \\\"keep_alive\\\": $keep_alive}\" \\\n        > /dev/null 2>&1 &\n}\n\necho \"=== Cohezion Model Preloader ===\"\necho \"Ollama: $OLLAMA_URL\"\necho \"\"\n\n# Tier 1: Always hot (keep_alive=-1 = indefinite)\necho \"Tier 1 — Always Hot (~5 GB):\"\npreload \"phi4-mini-reasoning\" -1\npreload \"nomic-embed-text\" -1\n\nif [[ \"${1:-}\" == \"--tier1-only\" ]]; then\n    wait\n    echo \"\"\n    echo \"Tier 1 loaded. Skipping Tier 2.\"\n    exit 0\nfi\n\n# Tier 2: Warm (keep_alive=-1 on preload, Ollama KEEP_ALIVE governs after use)\necho \"Tier 2 — Warm (~43 GB):\"\npreload \"glm-4.7-flash\" -1\npreload \"qwen3-coder:30b\" -1\n\nwait\necho \"\"\necho \"All Tier 1+2 models loaded.\"\necho \"Memory usage: $(free -h 2>/dev/null | grep Mem | awk '{print $3 \"/\" $2}' || echo 'N/A')\"\necho \"\"\necho \"Tier 3 models (loaded on-demand):\"\necho \"  - gpt-oss:20b (~16 GB)\"\necho \"  - deepcoder:14b (~12 GB)\"\necho \"  - nemotron-3-nano (~27 GB, 1M context)\"\n"
}
```

   750→class TestIntegrationOptimizationFullFlow:
   751→    """Integration tests for full optimization flow."""
   752→
   753→    def test_full_routing_optimization_flow(self):
   754→        """Test complete routing with optimization."""
   755→        reset_cost_aware_router()
   756→        tracker = SessionCostTracker("integration-test")
   757→        enforcer = BudgetEnforcer(budget_usd=10.0)
   758→
   759→        router = CostAwareRouter(
   760→            cost_tracker=tracker,
   761→            budget_enforcer=enforcer,
   762→            prefer_longer_models_if_cheaper_per_token=True,
   763→            cost_threshold=0.10,
   764→            latency_threshold=100.0,
   765→        )
   766→
   767→        # Route diverse queries
   768→        decisions = []
   769→        for query in [
   770→            "What is Python?",
   771→            "Write a function",
   772→            "Design a system",
   773→        ]:
   774→            decision, can_proceed = router.select_model(query)
   775→            decisions.append(decision)
   776→            assert can_proceed is True
   777→
   778→        # Record executions
   779→        for decision in decisions:
   780→            cost = router.record_execution(
   781→                decision.model,
   782→                decision.estimated_tokens,
   783→                100.0,
   784→            )
   785→            assert cost >= 0.0
   786→
   787→        # Get statistics
   788→        stats = router.get_statistics()
   789→        assert stats.total_queries == 3
   790→        assert stats.total_cost_usd >= 0.0
   791→

   793→class TestCostReductionTarget30Percent:
   794→    """Verify 30% cost reduction target with mixed workload."""

   796→    def test_cost_reduction_on_real_workload(self):

   803→            prefer_longer_models_if_cheaper_per_token=True,

   812→
   813→        for query in workload:

   815→            router.record_execution(decision.model, decision.estimated_tokens, 100.0)

     1→"""Non-blocking cost tracking module with batched async persistence.
     2→
     3→Features:
     4→- Per-session cost accumulation (<0.05ms overhead)
     5→- Batched async flush to vault (100 records/batch)
     6→- Graceful degradation on vault failure (in-memory fallback)
     7→- Per-model cost tracking
     8→- Session-level cost aggregation
     9→
    10→Architecture:
    11→  SessionCostTracker (in-memory)
    12→       ↓
    13→  Track usage (async, <0.05ms)
    14→       ↓
    15→  Batch flush (100 records/batch, async)
    16→       ↓
    17→  Vault persistence (best-effort, non-blocking)
    18→
    19→Usage:
    20→    tracker = SessionCostTracker.get_current()
    21→    cost_usd = tracker.track_usage_fast(
    22→        model="qwen3-coder:30b",
    23→        tokens=500,
    24→        duration_ms=250.0
    25→    )
    26→    # Cost is tracked, will flush asynchronously
    27→"""

    29→import asyncio
    30→import logging
    31→import time
    32→import uuid
    33→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    34→from typing import ClassVar, Optional
    35→
    36→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    37→
    38→
    39→@dataclass
    40→class CostRecord:
    41→    """Single cost tracking record (immutable after creation)."""
    42→
    43→    timestamp: float
    44→    session_id: str
    45→    model: str
    46→    tokens: int
    47→    duration_ms: float
    48→    cost_usd: float
    49→    record_id: str = field(default_factory=lambda: str(uuid.uuid4()))
    50→
    51→    def to_dict(self) -> dict:
    52→        """Convert to dictionary for serialization."""
    53→        return {
    54→            "timestamp": self.timestamp,
    55→            "session_id": self.session_id,
    56→            "model": self.model,
    57→            "tokens": self.tokens,
    58→            "duration_ms": self.duration_ms,
    59→            "cost_usd": self.cost_usd,
    60→            "record_id": self.record_id,
    61→        }
    62→
    63→
    64→class SessionCostTracker:
    65→    """Per-session cost accumulator with non-blocking async flush.
    66→
    67→    Design principles:
    68→    - Hot path: <0.05ms (in-memory tracking only)
    69→    - Batch flushes: 100 records/batch, async (non-blocking)
    70→    - Vault failures: Gracefully degrade to in-memory tracking
    71→    - Accuracy: ±1% (cost calculations include rounding)
    72→    """
    73→
    74→    _current_instance: ClassVar[Optional["SessionCostTracker"]] = None
    75→    _lock: ClassVar[asyncio.Lock] = asyncio.Lock()
    76→
    77→    def __init__(
    78→        self,
    79→        session_id: str,
    80→        model_costs: Optional[dict[str, float]] = None,
    81→        batch_size: int = 100,
    82→        vault_logger=None,
    83→    ):
    84→        """Initialize cost tracker.
    85→
    86→        Args:
    87→            session_id: Unique session identifier
    88→            model_costs: Dict mapping model names to cost per 1K tokens (default: standard rates)
    89→            batch_size: Records to batch before flushing (default: 100)
    90→            vault_logger: Optional vault persistence logger (non-blocking)
    91→        """
    92→        self.session_id = session_id
    93→        self.batch_size = batch_size
    94→        self.vault_logger = vault_logger
    95→
    96→        # Standard API costs (per 1K tokens)
    97→        # Local models (ollama) = $0.00
    98→        # API models: conservative estimates for unknown models
    99→        self.model_costs = model_costs or {
   100→            # Local models
   101→            "phi3:mini": 0.0,
   102→            "gemma3:4b": 0.0,
   103→            "mistral:7b": 0.0,
   104→            "llama4-scout": 0.0,
   105→            "qwen3-coder:32b": 0.0,
   106→            "deepseek-r1:8b": 0.0,
   107→            # API models (conservative estimates)
   108→            "gpt-4": 0.03,  # $0.03 per 1K tokens (input)
   109→            "gpt-4o": 0.015,  # $0.015 per 1K tokens
   110→            "claude-3-opus": 0.015,  # $0.015 per 1K tokens (input)
   111→            "claude-3-sonnet": 0.003,  # $0.003 per 1K tokens
   112→            "claude-3-haiku": 0.00025,  # $0.00025 per 1K tokens
   113→        }
   114→
   115→        # In-memory tracking (hot path)
   116→        self.records: list[CostRecord] = []
   117→        self.total_tokens = 0
   118→        self.total_cost_usd = 0.0
   119→        self.model_usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   120→        self.start_time = time.time()
   121→
   122→        # Flush state
   123→        self._flush_task: Optional[asyncio.Task] = None
   124→        self._pending_flush = False
   125→
   126→    @classmethod
   127→    def get_current(cls) -> Optional["SessionCostTracker"]:
   128→        """Get current session tracker (thread-safe)."""
   129→        return cls._current_instance
   130→
   131→    @classmethod
   132→    def set_current(cls, tracker: Optional["SessionCostTracker"]) -> None:
   133→        """Set current session tracker (thread-safe)."""
   134→        cls._current_instance = tracker
   135→
   136→    def track_usage_fast(
   137→        self,
   138→        model: str,
   139→        tokens: int,
   140→        duration_ms: float = 0.0,
   141→    ) -> float:
   142→        """Track usage in hot path (<0.05ms).
   143→
   144→        Args:
   145→            model: Model name
   146→            tokens: Tokens used
   147→            duration_ms: Request duration (optional, for analytics)
   148→
   149→        Returns:
   150→            Estimated cost in USD
   151→        """
   152→        # Calculate cost (in-memory, O(1))
   153→        cost_per_1k = self.model_costs.get(model, 0.015)  # Conservative default
   154→        cost_usd = (tokens / 1000.0) * cost_per_1k
   155→
   156→        # Record (in-memory, O(1) amortized)
   157→        record = CostRecord(
   158→            timestamp=time.time(),
   159→            session_id=self.session_id,
   160→            model=model,
   161→            tokens=tokens,
   162→            duration_ms=duration_ms,
   163→            cost_usd=cost_usd,
   164→        )
   165→        self.records.append(record)
   166→
   167→        # Accumulate totals (O(1))
   168→        self.total_tokens += tokens
   169→        self.total_cost_usd += cost_usd
   170→        self.model_usage[model] = self.model_usage.get(model, 0) + tokens
   171→
   172→        # Check if batch is ready for flush (non-blocking)
   173→        if len(self.records) >= self.batch_size and not self._pending_flush:
   174→            self._schedule_flush()
   175→
   176→        return cost_usd
   177→
   178→    def _schedule_flush(self) -> None:
   179→        """Schedule asynchronous flush (non-blocking, best-effort)."""
   180→        self._pending_flush = True
   181→        try:
   182→            # Only schedule if event loop is running
   183→            loop = asyncio.get_event_loop()
   184→            if loop.is_running():
   185→                loop.create_task(self._flush_batch())
   186→            else:
   187→                # Fallback: synchronous flush (shouldn't happen in async context)
   188→                self._flush_batch_sync()
   189→        except RuntimeError:
   190→            # No event loop running, fall back to sync
   191→            self._flush_batch_sync()
   192→
   193→    async def _flush_batch(self) -> None:
   194→        """Asynchronous batch flush (non-blocking)."""
   195→        if not self.records:
   196→            self._pending_flush = False
   197→            return
   198→
   199→        # Take snapshot (minimal lock time)
   200→        records_to_flush = self.records[: self.batch_size]
   201→        remaining = self.records[self.batch_size :]
   202→
   203→        try:
   204→            # Attempt vault persistence (best-effort, non-blocking)
   205→            if self.vault_logger:
   206→                try:
   207→                    # Non-blocking vault write (timeout + exception handling)
   208→                    await asyncio.wait_for(
   209→                        self.vault_logger.log_cost_records(records_to_flush),
   210→                        timeout=5.0,
   211→                    )
   212→                    # Success: remove flushed records
   213→                    self.records[:] = remaining
   214→                except (asyncio.TimeoutError, Exception) as e:
   215→                    # Vault failure: keep records in-memory
   216→                    logger.warning(
   217→                        f"Cost tracking vault flush failed: {e}. "
   218→                        f"Keeping {len(records_to_flush)} records in memory."
   219→                    )
   220→        finally:
   221→            self._pending_flush = False
   222→
   223→    def _flush_batch_sync(self) -> None:
   224→        """Synchronous fallback for flush (shouldn't be used normally)."""
   225→        if not self.records:
   226→            return
   227→
   228→        records_to_flush = self.records[: self.batch_size]
   229→        logger.debug(
   230→            f"Cost tracker: Synchronous flush of {len(records_to_flush)} records"
   231→        )
   232→        # In sync context, just log locally. Records stay in memory.
   233→        self._pending_flush = False
   234→
   235→    async def flush_all(self) -> int:
   236→        """Flush all remaining records to vault.
   237→
   238→        Returns:
   239→            Number of records flushed
   240→        """
   241→        if not self.records:
   242→            return 0
   243→
   244→        records_to_flush = self.records[:]
   245→        flushed_count = 0
   246→
   247→        try:
   248→            if self.vault_logger:
   249→                # Flush in batches
   250→                for i in range(0, len(records_to_flush), self.batch_size):
   251→                    batch = records_to_flush[i : i + self.batch_size]
   252→                    try:
   253→                        await asyncio.wait_for(
   254→                            self.vault_logger.log_cost_records(batch),
   255→                            timeout=5.0,
   256→                        )
   257→                        flushed_count += len(batch)
   258→                    except (asyncio.TimeoutError, Exception) as e:
   259→                        logger.warning(f"Batch flush failed: {e}")
   260→                        break
   261→
   262→        finally:
   263→            # Remove successfully flushed records
   264→            if flushed_count > 0:
   265→                self.records = self.records[flushed_count :]
   266→
   267→        return flushed_count
   268→
   269→    def get_session_cost(self) -> dict:
   270→        """Get session cost summary.
   271→
   272→        Returns:
   273→            Dictionary with total_cost_usd, total_tokens, model_usage, duration_sec
   274→        """
   275→        return {
   276→            "total_cost_usd": self.total_cost_usd,
   277→            "total_tokens": self.total_tokens,
   278→            "model_usage": self.model_usage.copy(),
   279→            "duration_sec": time.time() - self.start_time,
   280→            "pending_records": len(self.records),
   281→        }
   282→
   283→    def reset(self) -> None:
   284→        """Reset tracker (for testing)."""
   285→        self.records.clear()
   286→        self.total_tokens = 0
   287→        self.total_cost_usd = 0.0
   288→        self.model_usage.clear()
   289→
   290→
   291→def get_current_tracker() -> Optional[SessionCostTracker]:
   292→    """Get current session cost tracker."""
   293→    return SessionCostTracker.get_current()
   294→
   295→
   296→def set_current_tracker(tracker: Optional[SessionCostTracker]) -> None:
   297→    """Set current session cost tracker."""
   298→    SessionCostTracker.set_current(tracker)
   299→
   300→
   301→def reset_current_tracker() -> None:
   302→    """Reset current session tracker (testing only)."""
   303→    SessionCostTracker.set_current(None)
   304→

     1→"""Tests for cost/token tradeoff optimization in CostAwareRouter.
     2→
     3→Tests the core logic that prefers cheaper-per-token models when latency is acceptable.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import pytest
     7→from cohezion.swarm.cost_aware_router import (
     8→    CostAwareRouter,
     9→    QueryComplexity,
    10→)
    11→from cohezion.cost_optimization.cost_tracker import SessionCostTracker
    12→from cohezion.cost_optimization.budget_enforcer import BudgetEnforcer
    13→
    14→
    15→class TestCostTokenTradeoff:
    16→    """Test cost/token ratio optimization logic."""
    17→
    18→    @pytest.fixture
    19→    def router(self):
    20→        """Create router with token optimization enabled."""
    21→        router = CostAwareRouter(
    22→            cost_tracker=SessionCostTracker("test-tradeoff"),
    23→            budget_enforcer=BudgetEnforcer(budget_usd=100.0),
    24→            prefer_longer_models_if_cheaper_per_token=True,
    25→            cost_threshold=0.10,  # 10% cost difference threshold
    26→            latency_threshold=100.0,  # 100ms latency threshold
    27→        )
    28→        return router
    29→
    30→    def test_medium_query_prefers_phi3_if_tps_acceptable(self, router):
    31→        """Test that medium complexity queries prefer phi3 if TPS is acceptable."""
    32→        # Medium queries normally route to qwen (TPS: 8.0)
    33→        # But phi3 (TPS: 15.0) is much faster, so should be preferred if cost is equal
    34→
    35→        decision, _ = router.select_model("Write a Python function to sort data")
    36→
    37→        # With local models (cost=0), TPS becomes the deciding factor
    38→        # phi3 TPS (15.0) vs qwen TPS (8.0) → phi3 is 87.5% as fast (> threshold)
    39→        assert decision.model in ["phi3:mini", "qwen3-coder:32b"]
    40→
    41→    def test_optimization_tracking_via_swaps_counter(self):
    42→        """Test that token_optimization_swaps counter tracks optimization."""
    43→        router = CostAwareRouter(
    44→            cost_tracker=SessionCostTracker("swap-tracking"),
    45→            prefer_longer_models_if_cheaper_per_token=True,
    46→        )
    47→
    48→        initial_swaps = router.token_optimization_swaps
    49→
    50→        # Route queries that may trigger optimization
    51→        router.select_model("Write a Python function to process data")  # Medium
    52→        router.select_model("Design and optimize a distributed system")  # Complex
    53→
    54→        # Swaps counter should track optimization attempts
    55→        assert router.token_optimization_swaps >= initial_swaps
    56→
    57→    def test_cost_per_token_calculation_accuracy(self, router):
    58→        """Test accuracy of cost-per-token calculations."""
    59→        # All models are local (free), so cost per token should be 0
    60→        phi3_cost = router._get_cost_per_token("phi3:mini", 100)
    61→        qwen_cost = router._get_cost_per_token("qwen3-coder:32b", 100)
    62→        deepseek_cost = router._get_cost_per_token("deepseek-r1:8b", 100)
    63→
    64→        assert phi3_cost == 0.0
    65→        assert qwen_cost == 0.0
    66→        assert deepseek_cost == 0.0
    67→
    68→    def test_quality_score_reflects_selected_model(self, router):
    69→        """Test that quality score in decision matches selected model."""

     1→"""Tests for model selection optimization logic in CostAwareRouter.
     2→
     3→Tests the decision-making process for selecting between phi3, qwen, and deepseek.
     4→"""
     5→
     6→import pytest
     7→from cohezion.swarm.cost_aware_router import (
     8→    CostAwareRouter,
     9→    QueryComplexity,
    10→)
    11→from cohezion.cost_optimization.cost_tracker import SessionCostTracker
    12→
    13→
    14→class TestModelSelectionOptimization:
    15→    """Test model selection optimization logic."""
    16→
    17→    @pytest.fixture
    18→    def router(self):
    19→        """Create router with optimization enabled."""
    20→        router = CostAwareRouter(
    21→            cost_tracker=SessionCostTracker("test-selection"),
    22→            prefer_longer_models_if_cheaper_per_token=True,
    23→            cost_threshold=0.10,
    24→            latency_threshold=100.0,
    25→        )
    26→        return router
    27→
    28→    def test_simple_query_always_uses_phi3(self, router):
    29→        """Test that simple queries always use phi3:mini."""
    30→        simple_queries = [
    31→            "What is Python?",
    32→            "Define machine learning",
    33→            "List the items",
    34→        ]
    35→
    36→        for query in simple_queries:
    37→            decision, _ = router.select_model(query)
    38→            # Simple queries cannot be downgraded further, so always phi3 or from optimization
    39→            assert decision.model in ["phi3:mini", "qwen3-coder:32b"], f"Failed for: {query}"
    40→
    41→    def test_medium_query_optimizes_to_phi3_for_cost(self, router):
    42→        """Test that medium queries may downgrade to phi3 for cost savings."""
    43→        medium_queries = [
    44→            "Write a Python function",
    45→            "Create a simple data processing script",
    46→        ]
    47→
    48→        for query in medium_queries:
    49→            decision, _ = router.select_model(query)
    50→            # Medium queries may optimize to phi3 if TPS acceptable
    51→            assert decision.model in ["phi3:mini", "qwen3-coder:32b"], f"Failed for: {query}"
    52→
    53→    def test_complex_query_optimizes_to_faster_model_for_cost(self, router):
    54→        """Test that complex queries may optimize to faster models for cost/efficiency."""
    55→        complex_queries = [
    56→            "Design a distributed caching system",
    57→            "Implement production-grade security",
    58→        ]
    59→
    60→        for query in complex_queries:
    61→            decision, _ = router.select_model(query)
    62→            # Complex queries may optimize to qwen or phi3 if TPS/latency acceptable
    63→            assert decision.model in ["deepseek-r1:8b", "qwen3-coder:32b", "phi3:mini"], f"Failed for: {query}"
    64→
    65→    def test_complexity_analysis_drives_base_selection(self, router):
    66→        """Test that query complexity correctly determines base model selection."""
    67→        decision_simple, _ = router.select_model("What is this?")
    68→        assert decision_simple.complexity == QueryComplexity.SIMPLE
    69→
    70→        decision_medium, _ = router.select_model("Write a Python function")
    71→        assert decision_medium.complexity == QueryComplexity.MEDIUM
    72→
    73→        decision_complex, _ = router.select_model(
    74→            "Design and implement a distributed system with optimization"
    75→        )
    76→        assert decision_complex.complexity == QueryComplexity.COMPLEX
    77→
    78→    def test_optimization_maintains_quality_threshold(self, router):
    79→        """Test that optimization maintains minimum acceptable quality."""
    80→        queries = [
    81→            "What is Python?",  # Simple: phi3 (0.6)
    82→            "Write a function",  # Medium: phi3/qwen (0.6-0.85)
    83→            "Design a system",  # Complex: phi3/qwen/deepseek (0.6-0.95)
    84→        ]
    85→
    86→        quality_scores = []
    87→        for query in queries:
    88→            decision, _ = router.select_model(query)
    89→            quality_scores.append(decision.quality_score)
    90→
    91→        # Minimum quality should be phi3 (0.6)
    92→        min_quality = min(quality_scores)
    93→        assert min_quality >= 0.6, f"Quality score {min_quality} below phi3 minimum"
    94→
    95→        # All scores should be valid model quality scores
    96→        for score in quality_scores:
    97→            assert score in [0.6, 0.85, 0.95], f"Invalid quality score: {score}"
    98→
    99→    def test_model_selection_consistency(self, router):
   100→        """Test that identical queries get identical model selections."""
   101→        query = "Write a Python function to process data"
   102→
   103→        decisions = []
   104→        for _ in range(5):
   105→            decision, _ = router.select_model(query)
   106→            decisions.append(decision.model)
   107→
   108→        # All decisions should be identical for same query
   109→        assert len(set(decisions)) == 1, f"Inconsistent routing: {decisions}"
   110→
   111→    def test_routing_decision_reason_explains_choice(self, router):
   112→        """Test that routing decision includes explanatory reason."""
   113→        decision, _ = router.select_model("Write a Python function")
   114→
   115→        # Reason should explain the routing decision
   116→        assert "routed" in decision.reason.lower()
   117→        assert decision.model in decision.reason
   118→

    13→class TestCostThresholdTuning:

    16→    def test_cost_threshold_values_make_routing_decisions(self):
    17→        """Test that cost_threshold parameter is used in routing decisions."""

    24→        router_high = CostAwareRouter(

    35→        assert decision_low.model in ["phi3:mini", "qwen3-coder:32b"]

### **4. COST TRACKING ARCHITECTURE**

**Highly sophisticated, production-ready cost enforcement:**

#### BudgetEnforcer (`/src/cohezion/cost_optimization/budget_enforcer.py`)
- **Soft-stop policy**: Finish current task, block new ones
- **Progressive alerts**: 80%, 90%, 95%, 100% thresholds
- **Circuit breaker**: 3-strike emergency shutoff (prevents runaway costs)
- **Cached checks**: <0.5ms overhead (60s TTL)
- **Non-blocking**: Failures don't crash the system

- **Hot path**: <0.05ms in-memory tracking
- **Batched async flush**: 100 records/batch to vault
- **Graceful degradation**: Falls back to in-memory if vault fails
- **Per-model tracking**: Accumulates costs by model

## Team Orchestration & Swarm Execution Architecture - Comprehensive Exploration

Based on my thorough exploration of `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/`, here's the complete execution flow for team orchestration and swarm execution:

---

## 1. TEAMORCHESTRATOR: PLANNING PHASE

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/swarm/team_orchestrator.py`

### Core Data Structures

```python
@dataclass
class AgentSpec:
    """Claude Code agent specification"""
    name: str
    description: str
    tools: list[str] = ["Read", "Glob", "Grep", "Bash", "Edit", "Write"]
    disallowed_tools: list[str] = []
    model: str = "sonnet"
    instructions: str = ""
    ollama_model: str | None = None

@dataclass
class TaskSpec:
    """Individual task in a team plan"""
    id: str
    subject: str
    description: str
    assigned_to: str = ""
    blocked_by: list[str] = []  # Task dependencies
    tags: list[str] = []

@dataclass
class TeamPlan:
    """Complete team blueprint"""
    name: str
    intent: str
    agents: list[AgentSpec] = []
    tasks: list[TaskSpec] = []
    dependencies: dict[str, list[str]] = {}  # Task ID -> blocked_by IDs
```

### Main Method: `plan_team(intent, max_agents=4) -> TeamPlan`

**Execution Flow**:
1. **Search capability registry** for matching skills: `registry.find(intent, top_k=max_agents*2)`
2. **Separate & deduplicate** by type: filter to first `max_agents` skills
3. **Generate agent specs** from capabilities:
   - Infer role from skill name/tags (implementer, reviewer, researcher, tester)
   - Map roles to tool access: implementers get Read/Glob/Grep/Bash/Edit/Write
   - Map roles to Claude models: tester→haiku, implementer→sonnet, complex→opus
4. **Generate task list** in 3 phases:
   - Phase 1: Research/planning task (always assigned to agents[0])
   - Phase 2: Implementation tasks (one per agent, blocked_by=["t1"])
   - Phase 3: Integration/verification (blocked_by=all implementation task IDs)
5. **Build dependency map** from task.blocked_by lists

### Model Routing for Tasks

**Local Ollama Model Selection** (task-based):
- verify/test/lint → `phi4-mini-reasoning`
- code/implement/refactor → `qwen3-coder:30b`
- reason/architect/plan → `glm-4.7-flash`

**Claude Code Model Selection** (role-based):
- simple tasks → `haiku`
- moderate → `sonnet`
- complex → `opus`

---

## 2. EXECUTIONORCHESTRATOR: EXECUTION & PARALLELISM

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/swarm/execution_orchestrator.py`

### Key Data Structures

```python
@dataclass
class TaskResult:
    task_id: str
    subject: str
    status: str = "completed"  # "completed", "failed", "skipped"
    execution: ExecutionResult | None = None
    error: str | None = None
    duration_ms: float = 0.0

@dataclass
class ExecutionReport:
    report_id: str
    plan_name: str
    intent: str
    task_results: list[TaskResult] = []
    total_tokens: int = 0
    total_duration_ms: float = 0.0
    status: str = "completed"  # "completed", "partial", "failed"
```

### Main Method: `execute(plan: TeamPlan) -> ExecutionReport`

**Topological Sort to Execution Waves**:
```python
def _topological_sort(tasks: list[TaskSpec]) -> list[list[TaskSpec]]:
    """Group tasks into parallel-executable waves respecting dependencies"""
    completed: set[str] = set()
    waves: list[list[TaskSpec]] = []
    remaining = set(task_map.keys())
    
    while remaining:
        # Find all tasks whose dependencies are satisfied
        ready = [tid for tid in remaining 
                 if all(dep in completed for dep in task_map[tid].blocked_by)]
        if not ready:
            break  # Cycle detection
        waves.append([task_map[tid] for tid in sorted(ready)])
        completed.update(ready)
        remaining -= set(ready)
    return waves
```

**Parallel Execution Strategy**:
1. **For each wave**: Execute tasks in parallel via `asyncio.gather()`
2. **Capture results** in task_results list (preserves execution order)
3. **Accumulate metrics**: total_tokens, total_duration_ms
4. **Determine status**:
   - All completed → "completed"
   - Mix of completed/failed → "partial"
   - All failed → "failed"

### Two Execution Paths

**Path 1: Direct Execution** (`_execute_task_direct`):
- Looks up matching PRIME skill by tag
- Uses InstructionExpander to parse skill into ExecutablePlan
- Feeds to PlanExecutor for step-by-step execution
- Returns ExecutionResult

**Path 2: Compound Execution** (`_execute_task_compound`):
- Delegates to `TeamCompoundExecutor.execute_task(task)`
- Gets per-operation model routing
- Returns dict with skill_name, output, tokens, status
- Converts to TaskResult with ExecutionResult wrapper

---

## 3. TEAMCOMPOUNDEXECUTOR: BRIDGE LAYER

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/swarm/team_execution.py`

### Purpose
Connects ExecutionOrchestrator to CompoundExecutor for:
- Shared TemplateEngine (not per-task)
- Per-operation model routing
- Optional feedback loop integration

### Main Method: `execute_task(task: TaskSpec) -> dict`

```python
async def execute_task(self, task: TaskSpec) -> dict[str, Any]:
    # 1. Find matching PRIME skill by tag/keyword
    skill_name = self._find_skill_for_task(task)
    
    # 2. Delegate to CompoundExecutor with model routing
    result = await self.compound_executor.execute_skill(
        skill_name, task.description
    )
    
    # 3. Optional: Run feedback loop
    if self._auto_feedback:
        await self._run_feedback(skill_name, task.description)
    
    # 4. Return aggregated result
    return {
        "skill_name": skill_name,
        "output": result.final_output,
        "tokens": result.total_tokens,
        "duration_ms": elapsed_ms,
        "model": result.model_usage[0],
        "status": "completed",
        "steps": len(result.steps)
    }
```

---

## 4. COMPOUNDEXECUTOR: THE 11-STEP PIPELINE

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/compound/executor.py`

### 11-Step Execution Lifecycle

```
Step 1:   Get experience guidance from vault
Step 1.5: Parse request for alignment analysis (if enabled)
Step 2:   Log execution start to vault
Step 3:   Check input via guardrails
Step 4:   Execute task (via execute_fn callback)
          + Capture token metrics before/after
Step 5:   Check output via guardrails
Step 5.5: Analyze request-execution alignment (if enabled)
Step 6:   Extract execution patterns (if successful)
Step 7:   Refine skills based on execution results
Step 7.5: Check for degradation signals
Step 7.7: Record model quality metrics
Step 8:   Record execution metrics
Step 9:   Track journey in 12D FLUME universe
```

### Key Data Structure

```python
@dataclass
class ExecutionResult:
    success: bool
    output: str
    metrics: dict[str, Any]
    duration_seconds: float
    vault_experiment_path: str = ""
    vault_decision_paths: list[str] | None = None
    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
```

### Detailed Step-by-Step

**Step 1: Experience Guidance**
```python
guidance = self.get_experience_guidance(task_description, project)
# Returns: dict with relevant_context, patterns, decisions from vault
```

**Step 2: Execution Logging Start**
```python
experiment_path = self.logger.log_execution_start(ctx)
# Creates vault entry for tracking
```

**Steps 3-5: Guardrails**
- Input check: `guardrail_pipeline.check_input()` → blocks if suspicious
- Execute: `execute_fn(guidance)` → (output, metrics)
- Output check: `guardrail_pipeline.check_output()` → sanitizes if needed

**Step 6: Pattern Extraction**
```python
pattern_path = self.logger.extract_execution_pattern(
    source_path=experiment_path,
    pattern_name=f"{skill_name}_{operation_type}_success",
    description=f"Successful execution pattern for {skill_name}...",
    code_example=json.dumps(metrics, indent=2),
    domain="compound-engineering"
)
```

**Step 7: Skill Refinement**
```python
refined_path = self.skill_refiner.refine(
    skill_name=skill_name,
    operation_type=operation_type,
    execution_result=exec_result,
    patterns_extracted=decision_paths
)
```

**Step 8: Metrics Recording**
```python
self._metrics_collector.record_execution(
    skill_name=skill_name,
    success=success,
    tokens_used=token_metrics.get("tokens_used", 0),
    duration_ms=duration_seconds * 1000,
    model_used=token_metrics.get("model", "")
)
```

**Step 9: Journey Tracking (12D FLUME)**
```python
point = self._journey_tracker.track_execution(
    temp_result, task_description, operation_type
)
# Records position in 12D universe + coherence/efficiency metrics
```

## 5. INSTRUCTIONEXPANDER: SKILL → EXECUTABLE PLAN

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/core/instruction_expander.py`

### Data Structures

```python
@dataclass
class PlanStep:
    operation: str  # "search", "generate", "analyze", "transform", "persist"
    params: dict[str, Any]
    description: str

@dataclass
class ExecutablePlan:
    skill_name: str
    steps: list[PlanStep]
    domain: str
```

### Operation Classification

Parses PRIME skill instructions using keyword matching:
- **search**: find, locate, discover, identify, scan, lookup, query, check
- **generate**: create, write, compose, draft, produce, build, implement, seed
- **analyze**: evaluate, assess, examine, review, inspect, verify, validate, test
- **transform**: convert, format, extract, parse, map, offload, route, update
- **persist**: store, save, record, log, archive, walkthrough, provide

---

## 6. PLANEXECUTOR: STEP-BY-STEP EXECUTION

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/core/plan_executor.py`

### Main Method: `execute(plan: ExecutablePlan, input_text: str) -> ExecutionResult`

**Pipeline for Each Step**:
```
Step Input (context from previous step)
    ↓
Route to handler by operation type:
  - "generate"/"analyze" → _run_llm (calls token_client.generate)
  - "search" → _run_search (registry lookup)
  - "transform" → _run_transform (data mapping)
  - "persist" → _run_persist (vault write)
    ↓
Generate output
    ↓
Pipe as context to next step
```

**Token Tracking**:
- LLM operations: count output tokens (≈1 token per 4 chars)
- Search/transform: 0 tokens
- Accumulate total_tokens across all steps

## 7. SKILLSELECTOR: VAULT-GUIDED SKILL ROUTING

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/compound/skill_selector.py`

### Data Structure

```python
@dataclass
class SkillScore:
    skill_name: str
    coherence_score: float      # 0.0-1.0
    token_efficiency: float     # 0.0-1.0
    success_rate: float         # 0.0-1.0
    times_used: int
    composite_score: float      # Weighted: 50% coherence, 30% efficiency, 20% success
```

### Main Method: `select_skills(task_description, operation_type, project, top_k=3)`

1. Query vault for relevant patterns: `vault_find_relevant_context(task_description)`
2. Extract skill performance data from patterns
3. Compute composite score: `0.5*coherence + 0.3*efficiency + 0.2*success`
4. Sort by score (highest first)
5. Return top-k candidates

## 8. COSTAWAREROUTER: INTELLIGENT MODEL ROUTING

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py`

### Model Tier Strategy

**Local Ollama Models** (all $0.00 cost):
- **Tier 1 (Always-hot)**: phi4-mini-reasoning (3.8B Dense, 70 t/s)
- **Tier 2 (Warm)**: qwen3-coder:30b, glm-4.7-flash (30B MoE, ~25 t/s each)
- **Tier 3 (Cold)**: gpt-oss:20b, deepcoder:14b, nemotron-3-nano (legacy)

### Complexity Analysis

```python
class QueryComplexityAnalyzer:
    SIMPLE_KEYWORDS = {"what", "when", "where", "how many", "explain", "define"}
    COMPLEX_KEYWORDS = {"design", "implement", "build", "architecture", "debug", 
                        "optimize", "refactor", "production", "scalable", 
                        "distributed", "algorithm", "security", "performance"}
    
    def analyze(query: str) -> QueryComplexity:
        # Token estimation (1 token ≈ 4 chars)
        # Keyword matching (word boundaries)
        # Heuristic analysis: has_code, has_data_processing, has_logic, length
        
        if token_count < 10 and complex_matches == 0:
            return SIMPLE
        elif complex_matches >= 2 or (has_code and has_logic) or (is_long and has_data_processing):
            return COMPLEX
        else:
            return MEDIUM
```

### Model Selection Decision Tree

**Primary Selection by Complexity**:
- SIMPLE → phi4-mini-reasoning
- MEDIUM → qwen3-coder:30b
- COMPLEX → glm-4.7-flash

### Optimization: `select_model(query, max_cost_usd=None) -> (ModelRoutingDecision, can_proceed)`

**Key Method: `_optimize_model_selection`**

```python
def _optimize_model_selection(primary_model, complexity, estimated_tokens):
    # For COMPLEX queries:
    # - Check if qwen3-coder is cheaper per token AND latency acceptable
    # - If aggressive_cost_reduction enabled:
    #   - Also check phi4-mini (more lenient thresholds)
    
    # For MEDIUM queries:
    # - Check if phi4-mini acceptable
    # - If aggressive: be more lenient with 200ms latency increase
    
    # For SIMPLE queries:
    # - Strongly prefer phi4-mini if latency ≤150ms
```

**Latency Trade-off Thresholds**:
- phi4-mini-reasoning: 30ms (fastest)
- glm-4.7-flash: 80ms
- qwen3-coder:30b: 80ms
- gpt-oss:20b: 60ms
- deepcoder:14b: 120ms
- nemotron-3-nano: 100ms

**Cost/Token Optimization**:
```python
def _is_cheaper_with_acceptable_latency(
    candidate_model, primary_model, 
    candidate_cost_per_token, primary_cost_per_token,
    aggressive=False
):
    # For local models (all $0.00): use TPS as proxy
    # candidate_tps must be >= primary_tps * (1.0 - threshold)
    # threshold = 0.10 (standard) or 0.25 (aggressive)
    
    # Check latency trade-off
    latency_increase <= 150ms (standard) or 250ms (aggressive)
```

**Dynamic Threshold Tuning**:
- Track success rates for each model tier
- If phi4-mini success rate ≥85%: increase cost_threshold, relax latency
- If success rate <60%: decrease thresholds (be more conservative)

### Statistics Tracking

```python
@dataclass
class RoutingStatistics:
    total_queries: int
    simple_count: int
    medium_count: int
    complex_count: int
    fast_model_routed: int    # phi4-mini, phi3:mini
    medium_model_routed: int  # qwen, glm
    heavy_model_routed: int   # gpt-oss, deepcoder, nemotron
    total_cost_usd: float
    avg_cost_per_query: float
    cost_vs_deepseek_only: float  # Percentage savings
```

## 9. COMPOUNDDRIVER: FULL ORCHESTRATION CYCLE

**File**: `/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion-session-59/scripts/compound_driver.py`

### 5-Phase Compound Engineering Cycle

```
Phase 1: Select N PRIME skills
         └─ Parse all, prefer skills with instructions
         
Phase 2: Expand instructions → ExecutablePlan steps
         └─ Total steps = sum of steps across all skills
         
Phase 3: Execute via ExecutionOrchestrator
         └─ Build TeamPlan with expanded instructions
         └─ Run parallel task execution with dependency tracking
         
Phase 4: Retrospection (RetrospectionEngine)
         └─ analyze_execution(exec_report)
         └─ Calculate compound_score_delta
         
Phase 5: Skill Refinement (if compound_delta >= threshold)
         └─ suggest_skill_refinements()
         └─ Apply updates via SkillRefiner
```

### Main Entry: `run_compound_cycle(num_skills=5, threshold=0.5, dry_run=True, model="phi4-mini-reasoning")`

**Metrics Reported**:
- skills_selected, total_steps
- tasks_completed/failed, total_tokens
- execution_duration_ms
- compound_score_delta, patterns detected
- refinements_applied (skill versions updated)
- token_metrics (cache hit rate, tokens saved, total calls)

## 10. TASK DECOMPOSITION & ASSIGNMENT STRATEGY

### How Tasks Get to Agents

```
User Intent (string)
    ↓
TeamOrchestrator.plan_team()
    ├─ Search capability registry
    ├─ Generate AgentSpecs (role-based tool assignments)
    └─ Generate TaskList (3 phases, dependency-tracked)
    ↓
ExecutionOrchestrator.execute()
    ├─ Topological sort → execution waves
    └─ For each wave:
        ├─ Run tasks in parallel via asyncio.gather()
        └─ Collect results
    ↓
For each task:
    ├─ If TeamCompoundExecutor:
    │   ├─ Find skill by tag/keyword
    │   └─ execute_skill() with model routing
    └─ Else:
        ├─ InstructionExpander → ExecutablePlan
        └─ PlanExecutor → step-by-step execution
```

### Quality Assessment Flow

```
Execution Result
    ↓
Step 5: Guardrail checks (input/output)
    ├─ Input block → error + early return
    └─ Output sanitize/block → modify metrics
    ↓
Step 5.5: Alignment analysis
    ├─ Parse request intent
    ├─ Analyze alignment
    └─ Log misalignment (>0.3 score)
    ↓
Step 5 (continued): Anomaly detection (InflectionDetector)
    ├─ Check coherence (threshold 0.3)
    ├─ Check cache hit rate (threshold 0.2)
    ├─ Check token consumption trends
    ├─ Track failure streaks
    └─ CRITICAL severity → log inflection point
    ↓
Step 7.5: Degradation detection
    ├─ combined_hit_rate trend
    ├─ tokens_per_second trend
    ├─ mean_coherence trend
    └─ Log critical alerts
```

## 12. DATA FLOW EXAMPLE: Single Task Execution

```
UserRequest("Implement a cache system")
    ↓
TeamOrchestrator.plan_team()
    └─ PRIME_SKILL[CACHE_IMPLEMENTATION] → AgentSpec(name="cache-impl", role="implementer")
    └─ TaskSpec[t1] = "Research: cache patterns"
    └─ TaskSpec[t2] = "Implement: cache system" (blocked_by=["t1"])
    └─ TaskSpec[t3] = "Integration: run tests" (blocked_by=["t2"])
    ↓
ExecutionOrchestrator.execute(plan)
    └─ Wave 1: [t1] in parallel
        └─ _execute_task(t1)
            └─ TeamCompoundExecutor.execute_task()
                ├─ Find skill: RESEARCH_PATTERNS
                └─ CompoundExecutor.execute_task()
                    ├─ Step 1: vault guidance for "research cache patterns"
                    ├─ Step 2: log start
                    ├─ Step 3: input guardrails check
                    ├─ Step 4: invoke execute_fn (LLM call via token_client)
                    ├─ Step 5: output guardrails
                    ├─ Step 6: extract pattern
                    ├─ Step 7: refine skill
                    ├─ Step 8: record metrics
                    └─ Step 9: track journey
                └─ Return ExecutionResult
        └─ Return TaskResult[t1]
    ↓
    └─ Wave 2: [t2] in parallel (now unblocked)
        └─ Execute CACHE_IMPLEMENTATION_PRIME skill...
    ↓
    └─ Wave 3: [t3] in parallel
        └─ Execute TEST_INTEGRATION_PRIME skill...
    ↓
ExecutionOrchestrator.execute() returns ExecutionReport
    ├─ task_results: [TaskResult[t1], TaskResult[t2], TaskResult[t3]]
    ├─ status: "completed" (all tasks successful)
    ├─ total_tokens: 1450 (sum across all tasks)
    └─ total_duration_ms: 3200.5
```

## 13. CRITICAL INSIGHTS: HOW WORK IS DISTRIBUTED

### 1. **Task Dependency Awareness**
- Tasks are NOT assigned randomly
- ExecutionOrchestrator respects blocked_by dependencies
- Parallel execution only within the same wave (independent tasks)
- Topological sort guarantees dependencies are satisfied before execution

### 2. **Model Routing Layers**
- **TeamOrchestrator**: Maps role → Claude Code model (haiku/sonnet/opus)
- **CostAwareRouter**: Maps query complexity → local Ollama model
- **SkillSelector**: Uses vault patterns to pick best skill for operation_type
- **DynamicModelRouter**: Memory-aware concurrent model management

### 3. **Quality Gates**
- **Guardrails**: Block/sanitize input/output
- **Alignment Analyzer**: Check request-execution coherence
- **Inflection Detector**: Anomaly detection (coherence, cache hit, token trends)
- **Degradation Detector**: Monitor metric trends over time

### 4. **Non-Blocking Failures**
All quality checks are **non-blocking** (wrapped in try/except):
- Guardrails failure → logs warning, continues
- Alignment analysis failure → logs debug, continues
- Skill refinement failure → logs debug, continues
- Journey tracking failure → logs debug, continues

This ensures execution never fails due to observability infrastructure.

### 5. **Compound Scoring**
TeamExecutor computes: `0.6*success_rate + 0.25*avg_coherence + 0.15*avg_efficiency`

This drives skill refinement decisions in the RetrospectionEngine.

---

## 14. EXECUTION CORRECTNESS GUARANTEES

1. **Atomicity**: Each task is atomic (succeeds or fails, no partial states)
2. **Dependency Ordering**: Tasks never execute before their dependencies complete
3. **Parallelism**: Independent tasks within a wave execute concurrently
4. **Idempotency**: Token metrics are deltas (before/after snapshots)
5. **Observable**: Every step logged to vault for retrospection
6. **Deterministic Routing**: Same query complexity always gets same model (within algorithm constraints)

This architecture enables **compound engineering**: each execution produces learnings that improve future executions through vault-guided skill selection and refinement.

## Comprehensive PRIME Skill System & Compound Loop Exploration Report

I've thoroughly explored the PRIME skill system, instruction expansion, skill refinement, multi-agent consensus voting, and 12D journey tracking in the Cohezion compound engineering architecture. Here's the complete lifecycle and data structures:

---

### 1. PRIME Skill Definitions (Markdown Structure)

**Location**: `src/cohezion/skills/` (124 total skills, mix of `.md` files and subdirectories)

**Example PRIME Skills Found**:
- `RELIABILITY_PRIME.md` - Circuit breakers, connection pooling, graceful degradation
- `MODEL_ROUTING_PRIME.md` - Ollama model orchestration with memory-aware scheduling
- `SYSTEM_GUARDRAILS_PRIME.md` - Security pipeline (constitutional checks, injection detection, rate limiting)
- `RESEARCH_PATTERNS_PRIME.md` - 12D trajectory physics and coherence laws
- `QUARTER_ON_A_STRING_PRIME.md`, `TENSOR_NETWORK_OPS_PRIME.md` - Specialized operations

**Standard Structure**:
```
# SKILL: [NAME]
## DOMAIN EXPERTISE
  [Specialist context]
## KEY CONCEPTS / TEXTS
  - **ConceptName** - Description
## INSTRUCTION
  1. [Numbered step with operation type]
  2. [Next step]
  ...
## VERSION
v0.1
## SEE ALSO
  - [Related PRIME skills]
```

**Key Fields**:
- **DOMAIN EXPERTISE**: Full text context for the specialist agent
- **INSTRUCTIONS**: Numbered list (1-10+), each instruction is classified by operation type
- **CONCEPTS**: Key domain concepts with descriptions
- **VERSION**: Semantic versioning for skill refinement tracking

---

### 2. SkillSpec & Template Engine (Structured Parsing)

**File**: `src/cohezion/core/template_engine.py`

**SkillSpec Dataclass**:
```python
@dataclass
class SkillSpec:
    name: str                          # e.g., "COMPOUND_ENGINEERING_PRIME"
    domain_expertise: str              # Full DOMAIN EXPERTISE section text
    concepts: dict[str, str]           # Extracted key concepts
    instructions: list[str]            # Numbered top-level steps (extracted via regex)
    version: str                       # "v0.1" etc., or "unknown"
    see_also: list[str]               # Related skill names
    raw_content: str                   # Complete markdown source
    source_path: Path                  # Filesystem location
```

**Parsing Pipeline**:
1. `_extract_sections()` - Split markdown by `## HEADING` markers
2. `_parse_concepts()` - Extract `- **Name** - Description` bullet lists
3. `_parse_instructions()` - Extract top-level numbered steps (1., 2., 3., etc.)
4. `_parse_see_also()` - Extract skill references from SEE ALSO section

---

### 3. Instruction Expander (PRIME → Executable Plan)

**File**: `src/cohezion/core/instruction_expander.py`

**Two Key Classes**:

#### PlanStep (Atomic Unit)
```python
@dataclass
class PlanStep:
    operation: str          # One of: "search", "generate", "analyze", "transform", "persist"
    params: dict[str, Any]  # Extracted parameters (references, concepts)
    description: str        # Original instruction text
```

#### ExecutablePlan (Ordered Sequence)
```python
@dataclass
class ExecutablePlan:
    skill_name: str         # Source skill name
    steps: list[PlanStep]   # Ordered sequence of executable steps
    domain: str             # Inherited from SkillSpec.domain_expertise
```

**Operation Classification** (Keyword Matching):
- **SEARCH**: `search`, `find`, `locate`, `discover`, `query`, `check`
- **GENERATE**: `generate`, `create`, `write`, `build`, `implement`, `seed`
- **ANALYZE**: `analyze`, `evaluate`, `assess`, `review`, `verify`, `test`
- **TRANSFORM**: `transform`, `convert`, `format`, `extract`, `parse`, `route`, `update`
- **PERSIST**: `store`, `save`, `persist`, `record`, `log`, `archive`

**Parameter Extraction**:
- Backtick-quoted identifiers: `` `reference` `` → `params["references"]`
- Bold-quoted terms: `**concept**` → `params["concepts"]`
- Operation-specific: `persist` → `params["persist_type"] = "log"`

---

### 4. Skill Refiner (Learning from Execution)

**File**: `src/cohezion/compound/skill_refiner.py`

**ExecutionMetrics** (Extracted from ExecutionResult):
```python
@dataclass
class ExecutionMetrics:
    success: bool           # Did execution succeed?
    duration_seconds: float # Wall-clock time
    tokens_used: int        # LLM tokens consumed
    token_efficiency: float # tokens_used / duration_seconds
    quality_score: float    # 1.0 - anomaly_score
    anomaly_score: float    # 0.0-1.0 (lower = better)
    cached_hits: int        # Number of cache hits
```

**LearningSignal** (Extracted from ExecutionMetrics):
```python
@dataclass
class LearningSignal:
    skill_name: str         # Which skill was refined?
    operation_type: str     # generate/analyze/search/etc
    key_insight: str        # What was learned
    metric_change: str      # How did metrics change?
    recommendation: str     # What to do next
    confidence: float       # 0.0-1.0 confidence in signal
```

**Refine Workflow**:
1. Extract metrics from execution result
2. Skip if execution failed
3. Generate learning signal (only if high quality)
4. Append refinement to PRIME skill `.md` file
5. Bump version number
6. Non-blocking persistence to vault

---

### 5. Retrospection Engine (Pattern Analysis)

**File**: `src/cohezion/core/compound/retrospection.py`

**LearningPattern** (Parsed from KEY_LEARNINGS.md):
```python
@dataclass
class LearningPattern:
    id: int                        # Learning identifier (e.g., 1, 2, 3)
    title: str                     # Human-readable title
    date: str                      # ISO date (YYYY-MM-DD) if present
    tags: list[str]               # Brane encodings: ["brane-1", "brane-2"]
    cross_references: list[str]    # References to other learnings/skills
```

**SkillRefinement** (Suggested Updates):
```python
@dataclass
class SkillRefinement:
    skill_name: str               # Which PRIME skill to refine
    reason: str                   # Why refinement is suggested
    suggested_additions: list[str] # Learning titles that inform it
```

**Compound Score Calculation**:
- Count incoming references from other learnings
- Weight by outgoing references (0.3 multiplier)
- Normalize to 0.0-1.0 range
- Score = `(incoming_refs / max_refs) + 0.3 * (outgoing_refs / total_learnings)`

---

### 6. Multi-Agent Skill Consensus Voting

**File**: `src/cohezion/compound/skill_consensus_voter.py`

**AgentVote** (Single Agent's Vote):
```python
@dataclass
class AgentVote:
    agent_id: str                         # Voting agent identifier
    task_description: str                 # What task this vote addresses
    operation_type: str                   # generate/analyze/search/etc
    voted_skills: list[SkillScore]       # Ranked skills from this agent
    agent_coherence_score: float = 0.5    # Historical coherence for weighting
```

**SkillScore** (Candidate Skill):
```python
@dataclass
class SkillScore:
    skill_name: str              # Skill identifier
    coherence_score: float       # 0.0-1.0 quality metric
    token_efficiency: float      # 0.0-1.0 token efficiency
    success_rate: float          # 0.0-1.0 historical success
    times_used: int              # Number of successful uses
    composite_score: float       # Weighted combination
```

**ConsensusResult** (Voting Outcome):
```python
@dataclass
class ConsensusResult:
    consensus_skill: SkillScore | None     # Winning skill
    confidence_score: float                # 0.0-1.0
    strategy_used: VotingStrategy          # MAJORITY/WEIGHTED/UNANIMOUS
    votes_for_consensus: int               # How many agents agreed
    total_votes: int                       # Total agents voting
    runner_up_skill: SkillScore | None     # Second place
    fallback_used: bool                    # True if consensus failed
    vote_aggregation: dict[str, Any]      # Debug metadata
```

**Voting Strategies**:
1. **MAJORITY**: >50% agreement on top-rated skill
2. **WEIGHTED**: Sum of (agent_coherence_score × vote) > 50% threshold
3. **UNANIMOUS**: All agents must vote for same skill
4. **FALLBACK**: Ranked scoring across all agent votes if consensus fails

**Performance**: O(N×K) where N=agents, K=top-k skills. Typical: <10ms for 5 agents × 5 skills.

### 7. Journey Tracker (12D FLUME Trajectories)

**File**: `src/cohezion/compound/journey_tracker.py`

**OperationType Enum** (Operation-Specific Modulation):
```python
class OperationType(Enum):
    GENERATE = "generate"      # High novelty (0) + logic (1)
    ANALYZE = "analyze"        # High logic (1) + field (2)
    SEARCH = "search"          # High spatial (3)
    TRANSFORM = "transform"    # Moderate all dimensions
    PERSIST = "persist"        # High temporal (4) + precipitation (5)
```

**TrajectoryPoint** (Single 12D Position):
```python
@dataclass
class TrajectoryPoint:
    dimensions: np.ndarray             # 12D vector [-1, 1] normalized
    timestamp: float                   # Unix timestamp
    coherence: float                   # 0.0-1.0 quality metric
    efficiency: float                  # 0.0-1.0 token efficiency
    operation_type: str                # "generate", "analyze", etc
    task_description: str              # What task generated this
    metadata: dict[str, Any] = None    # Additional context
```

**Journey** (Complete Trajectory):
```python
@dataclass
class Journey:
    execution_id: str                  # Unique execution identifier
    points: list[TrajectoryPoint]      # Ordered trajectory points
    start_time: float                  # When execution started
    end_time: float                    # When execution completed
    task_description: str              # Human-readable task
    operation_type: str                # Operation classification
    final_success: bool                # Did execution succeed?
    phi_score: float                   # Trajectory quality score
```

**12D Axiomatic Dimensions**:
1. Novelty (0) - How creative/novel is the output?
2. Logic (1) - How logically sound?
3. Field (2) - Domain expertise alignment
4. Spatial (3) - Geometric/structural coherence
5. Temporal (4) - Time complexity efficiency
6. Precipitation (5) - Value/impact realized
7. Coherence (6) - Internal consistency
8. Efficiency (7) - Resource utilization
9. Convergence (8) - Toward goal state
10. Smoothness (9) - Trajectory smoothness
11. Resonance (10) - Harmonic alignment with system
12. Harmony (11) - Overall balance

**Projection Pipeline**:
1. `_text_to_latent()` - SHA-256 hash → 2048D embedding (deterministic)
2. `_holographic_project()` - 2048D → 16D chunks → 12D via interpolation
3. `_step_to_axiomatic()` - Apply operation-specific modulation profiles
4. Cache results for deterministic reproducibility

**Modulation Profiles** (12D vectors per operation):
```python
# GENERATE: High novelty (0.9) + logic (0.8)
# ANALYZE: High logic (0.9) + field (0.8)
# SEARCH: High spatial (0.9)
# TRANSFORM: Moderate all (0.6 across all dims)
# PERSIST: High temporal (0.9) + precipitation (0.8)
```

**Quality Scoring**:
```
phi_score = coherence × 0.5 + smoothness × 0.3 + convergence × 0.2
```

**Execution Lifecycle**:
1. **Experience Guidance** - Query vault for similar prior runs
2. **Alignment Analysis** - Check request coherence vs available skills
3. **Execution** - Run task with token-efficient client + guardrails
4. **Metrics Collection** - Record coherence, efficiency, cache hit rate
5. **Journey Tracking** - Map to 12D FLUME trajectory
6. **Anomaly Detection** - Check for critical inflection points
7. **Feedback Loop** - Detect failures, attempt intelligent retries
8. **Skill Refinement** - Extract learnings, update PRIME skill definition
9. **Pattern Extraction** - Save reusable insights to vault
10. **Metrics Persistence** - Record execution timeline and trends

**CompoundExecutor Constructor** (46 optional parameters):

    enable_guardrails: bool = True,
    inflection_detector: InflectionDetector = None,

    enable_skill_refinement: bool = True,
    metrics_collector: CompoundMetricsCollector = None,
    journey_tracker: JourneyTracker = None,
    journey_persistence: JourneyPersistence = None,
    alignment_analyzer: RequestAlignmentAnalyzer = None,
    enable_alignment_analysis: bool = False,
    degradation_detector: DegradationDetector = None,
    model_quality_classifier: ModelQualityClassifier = None,
)
```

### 9. Compound Feedback Loop (Retry Logic)

**File**: `src/cohezion/compound/feedback_loop.py`

**RetryStrategy**:
```python
class RetryStrategy(Enum):
    SAME_SKILL = "same_skill"               # Retry identical
    ALTERNATIVE_SKILL = "alternative_skill" # Try different skill
    ADJUSTED_PARAMETERS = "adjusted_parameters"  # Same skill, new params
    ESCALATE_MODEL = "escalate_model"       # Use more capable model
```

**RetryAttempt**:
```python
@dataclass
class RetryAttempt:
    attempt_number: int                    # 1, 2, 3, etc
    strategy: RetryStrategy                # How was this retry different?
    skill_used: str                        # Which skill was tried?
    success: bool                          # Did it work?
    anomaly_detected: AnomalyDetection = None  # What went wrong?
    execution_result: ExecutionResult = None   # Full result
    error: str = ""                        # Error message
```

**FeedbackLoopResult**:
```python
@dataclass
class FeedbackLoopResult:
    task_description: str                  # Original task
    operation_type: str                    # Operation classification
    success: bool                          # Final outcome
    final_output: str                      # Best output obtained
    final_metrics: dict[str, Any]         # Final performance metrics
    attempts: list[RetryAttempt]          # All retry attempts
    total_retries: int                     # How many retries?
    total_duration_seconds: float          # Total wall-clock time
    learned_skill_adjustment: str          # What was learned?
    should_persist_learning: bool          # Save to vault?
```

**Feedback Loop Lifecycle**:
1. Execute task with CompoundExecutor
2. Detect anomalies using InflectionDetector
3. If CRITICAL (score > threshold): trigger retry
4. Try alternative strategy (different skill, adjusted params, escalated model)
5. Log retry trajectory and learning
6. Refine skills based on cumulative feedback
7. Return final result with all retry metadata

### 12. Data Quality & Coherence Tracking

**Key Metrics Tracked**:
- `coherence_score` (0.0-1.0) - Internal consistency quality
- `token_efficiency` (tokens / duration) - Resource utilization
- `anomaly_score` (0.0-1.0) - Deviation from expected behavior
- `quality_score` (1.0 - anomaly_score) - Inverse of anomaly
- `cache_hit_rate` (0.0-1.0) - Token reuse efficiency
- `success_rate` (0.0-1.0) - Skill reliability over time
- `phi_score` - 12D trajectory quality (coherence × 0.5 + smoothness × 0.3 + convergence × 0.2)

**Coherence Thresholds** (from RESEARCH_PATTERNS_PRIME):
- `< 0.70`: "Trajectory collapse" risk (HIHO boundary)
- `0.75`: Safe minimum for reliable reasoning
- `0.80`: High-fidelity spacetime derivation
- `0.85`: Optimal operational range

## Long-Horizon Task Plan: ModelPoolManager + Adversarial Review Pipeline

Below is the complete, actionable implementation plan for Session 59.

---

### Part 1: Feature Design -- ModelPoolManager

**What it is.** A lifecycle manager for the 3-tier (hot/warm/cold) model rotation. It dynamically loads, unloads, health-checks, and memory-schedules local Ollama models so that `CostAwareRouter` can make routing decisions against actual model availability rather than static assumptions.

**Why it matters.** Right now `CostAwareRouter.select_model()` picks from a static dictionary of models (lines 230-241 of `cost_aware_router.py`). It has no idea whether the model is actually loaded. `DynamicModelRouter` has memory analysis but operates with a completely separate model registry. `OllamaModelManager` in `model_manager.py` does benchmarking and metrics but has no tier-awareness or lifecycle management. The `preload_models.sh` script does tier-based loading but is a one-shot script with no runtime integration. `ModelPoolManager` unifies these: it becomes the authoritative runtime source of truth for model availability, integrating memory pressure from `MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer`, metrics from `OllamaModelManager`, and tier policy from the preload script.

### File-by-File Implementation Breakdown

#### New Files to Create

**1. `src/cohezion/swarm/model_pool_manager.py`** (core implementation)

This is the main deliverable. It should contain:

```
class ModelTierPolicy(Enum):
    HOT = "hot"      # Tier 1: always loaded, keep_alive=-1
    WARM = "warm"    # Tier 2: loaded on startup, evictable under pressure
    COLD = "cold"    # Tier 3: loaded on-demand, evicted after timeout

@dataclass
class PooledModel:
    name: str
    tier: ModelTierPolicy
    size_gb: float
    loaded: bool = False
    last_used: float = 0.0
    health_status: str = "unknown"  # "healthy", "degraded", "failed", "unknown"
    load_count: int = 0
    error_count: int = 0
    avg_latency_ms: float = 0.0

class ModelPoolManager:
    """Lifecycle manager for 3-tier hot/warm/cold model rotation.

    Responsibilities:
    - Track which models are loaded vs available
    - Health-check loaded models (ping /api/generate with trivial prompt)
    - Auto-promote cold -> warm when frequently used
    - Auto-demote warm -> cold under memory pressure
    - Enforce global concurrency limit (4 models max)
    - Provide CostAwareRouter with live availability data
    """
```

Key methods:
- `__init__(self, ollama_host, tier_config, max_concurrent)` -- Initialize with tier definitions from the preload script's 3-tier structure
- `async def initialize(self)` -- Query Ollama `/api/tags` to discover loaded models, reconcile with tier config
- `async def ensure_loaded(self, model_name) -> bool` -- Load model if not loaded; evict cold models if memory pressured
- `async def health_check(self, model_name) -> PooledModel` -- Ping model with trivial prompt, update health status
- `async def health_check_all(self) -> dict[str, PooledModel]` -- Health check all loaded models
- `async def get_available_models(self) -> list[PooledModel]` -- Return models currently loaded and healthy
- `async def evict_model(self, model_name)` -- Unload model via keep_alive=0
- `async def promote(self, model_name, target_tier)` -- Change tier policy (cold -> warm, etc.)
- `async def demote_under_pressure(self)` -- Evict lowest-priority models when memory > 80%
- `def get_pool_status(self) -> PoolStatus` -- Summary dataclass with loaded/available/memory stats
- Uses `@safe_singleton` pattern from `cohezion.concurrency.safe_singleton`
- Integrates `MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer` from `dynamic_model_router.py` for memory pressure

The tier configuration should default to matching the `preload_models.sh` structure:
```python
DEFAULT_TIER_CONFIG = {
    "phi4-mini-reasoning": ModelTierPolicy.HOT,
    "nomic-embed-text": ModelTierPolicy.HOT,
    "glm-4.7-flash": ModelTierPolicy.WARM,
    "qwen3-coder:30b": ModelTierPolicy.WARM,
    "gpt-oss:20b": ModelTierPolicy.COLD,
    "deepcoder:14b": ModelTierPolicy.COLD,
    "nemotron-3-nano": ModelTierPolicy.COLD,
}
```

    available_names = {m.name for m in available}
    if primary_model not in available_names:
        # Fallback to next-best available model in same tier
        model = self._find_available_fallback(primary_model, available_names)
```

This is a minimal, backward-compatible change. When `pool_manager` is None (the default), behavior is identical to today.

---

### Model Assignment Rationale

| Task | Assigned Model | Rationale |
|------|---------------|-----------|
| T1: Data models + enums | phi4-mini-reasoning | Simple dataclass/enum definitions, no heavy reasoning needed. 70 t/s throughput makes this fast. |
| T2: Architecture + PRIME skill | glm-4.7-flash | Architectural planning and design documentation requires deep reasoning. GLM's 0.90 quality score matches. |
| T3: Core class implementation | qwen3-coder:30b | 200+ lines of async Python with httpx integration. Code generation is qwen3-coder's strength (0.88 quality). |
| T4: Config schema | phi4-mini-reasoning | Pydantic config is formulaic. Small model handles this efficiently. |
| T5: Router integration | qwen3-coder:30b | Modifying existing async code paths requires code-aware model. |
| T6: Health check + promotion | qwen3-coder:30b | Complex async logic with state machines (health transitions, promotion). |
| T7: Tests | phi4-mini-reasoning | Test patterns are established in the codebase; follows templates. |
| T8: Lint pass | Claude haiku | Quick surface-level review -- haiku's speed is ideal. |
| T9: Deep code review | Claude sonnet | Logic errors, edge cases, security -- sonnet's balanced depth. |
| T10: Architectural review | Claude opus | Design coherence, charter alignment, compound engineering -- opus's strength. |

---

### Part 2: Adversarial Review Protocol

#### Tier 1 -- Quick Lint Pass (Claude haiku)

**Scope:** Surface-level quality gate. Should complete in under 60 seconds of model time.

**Checks:**
1. **Import hygiene** -- Are all imports used? Any circular imports? Do new modules have `__init__.py` exports?
2. **Type hints** -- Are all public function signatures fully annotated? Any `Any` that should be specific?
3. **Docstring completeness** -- NumPy-style docstrings on all classes and public methods?
4. **Style compliance** -- Line length, naming conventions, ruff-compatible formatting?
5. **Test coverage** -- Does every public method have at least one test? Test count target: minimum 12 tests.
6. **Obvious errors** -- Missing `await` on async calls, bare `except`, unused variables?

**Verdict format:**
```
TIER 1 VERDICT: PASS / FAIL
Issues found: N
Blockers: [list of must-fix items]
Warnings: [list of should-fix items]
```

**Pass criteria:** Zero blockers. Warnings are informational.

#### Tier 2 -- Deep Code Review (Claude sonnet)

**Scope:** Logic correctness, edge cases, security. Should take 2-3 minutes of model time.

**Checks:**
1. **Concurrency safety** -- Are there race conditions in `ensure_loaded()` when multiple tasks request the same model simultaneously? Is the singleton thread-safe?
2. **Error handling** -- What happens when Ollama is unreachable? When a model fails to load? When health check times out? Are all error paths covered?
3. **Resource leaks** -- Are httpx clients properly closed? Are asyncio tasks cleaned up?
4. **Edge cases:**
   - What if all models are unhealthy?
   - What if memory pressure is at 100%?
   - What if `evict_model` fails (model stuck)?
   - What if tier config contains a model not installed in Ollama?
   - What if two models have the same name but different tags?
5. **API correctness** -- Does the Ollama `/api/generate` with `keep_alive=0` actually unload? Is `/api/tags` the right endpoint for discovery?
6. **Backward compatibility** -- Does the CostAwareRouter change break any existing tests? Is the pool_manager parameter truly optional?
7. **Security** -- Can a malicious model name cause injection? Are timeouts enforced?

**Verdict format:**
```
TIER 2 VERDICT: PASS / CONDITIONAL_PASS / FAIL
Critical issues: [must-fix before merge]
Important issues: [should-fix within session]
Minor issues: [can address later]
Risk assessment: LOW / MEDIUM / HIGH
```

**Pass criteria:** Zero critical issues. Important issues must have a documented plan.

#### Tier 3 -- Architectural Review (Claude opus)

**Scope:** Design coherence, integration quality, charter/constitution alignment. 3-5 minutes of model time.

**Checks:**
1. **Compound Engineering alignment** -- Does `ModelPoolManager` make future features easier? Does it integrate with the compound loop (executor -> metrics -> journey -> refinement)?
2. **HIHO coherence** -- Does the tier promotion/demotion maintain the 0.5 coherence baseline? Are state transitions reversible?
3. **Charter compliance:**
   - Observable AI: Are all state transitions logged? Can you inspect pool state externally?
   - Deterministic Responsibility: Are operations idempotent? Can you replay from checkpoints?
   - Constitution: No WMD uplift risk, honest metrics, human oversight preserved?
4. **Integration coherence:**
   - How does `ModelPoolManager` relate to `DynamicModelRouter`? Overlap? Conflict? Should `DynamicModelRouter.active_models` be replaced by pool state?
   - How does it relate to `OllamaModelManager`? Should metrics be shared?
   - Does it work with `DynamicConcurrencyGate`'s concurrency limits?
   - Does it interact correctly with `ResilientOllamaClient`'s circuit breaker?
5. **Data storage alignment** -- Does pool state follow the 3-tier storage strategy (git/SurrealDB/external)?
6. **Journey tracking** -- Should `ModelPoolManager` emit journey events for load/evict/promote? If so, what phase?
7. **Token efficiency** -- Is the health check overhead justified? What's the cost of periodic pings vs the benefit of knowing model health?
8. **Expert Domain Lattice fit** -- Which expert stream owns this? (Answer: Engineer, with Architect oversight)

**Verdict format:**
```
TIER 3 VERDICT: PASS / CONDITIONAL_PASS / FAIL
Architectural concerns: [design-level issues]
Integration risks: [cross-module conflicts]
Charter compliance: ALIGNED / NEEDS_WORK
Compound engineering score: 0.0-1.0
Recommendations: [ordered list]
```

**Pass criteria:** No architectural concerns rated as blocking. Charter compliance must be ALIGNED. Compound engineering score >= 0.7.

#### Combined Verdict Logic

```
FINAL VERDICT:
  IF any tier = FAIL -> FAIL (with specific remediation)
  IF all tiers = PASS -> PASS
  IF any tier = CONDITIONAL_PASS -> CONDITIONAL_PASS (list conditions)

MERGE READINESS:
  PASS -> Ready to merge
  CONDITIONAL_PASS -> Merge after conditions addressed
  FAIL -> Do not merge, rework required
```

    28→class IDEPriority(Enum):
    29→    """IDE priority weights based on user preference and system impact"""
    30→
    31→    ANTIGRAVITY = 3  # Maximum priority for agentic development
    32→    ZED = 2  # High priority for interactive development
    33→    OPENCODE = 1  # Standard priority for CLI operations
