    36→class ModelTier(Enum):
    37→    """Memory-bandwidth-aware model tiers optimized for DDR5 constraints"""
    38→
    39→    MICRO = (0.5, 4, 12)  # 0.5-4GB, 12 concurrent, 15-20 t/s
    40→    SMALL = (4, 8, 8)  # 4-8GB, 8 concurrent, 8-15 t/s
    41→    MEDIUM = (8, 16, 4)  # 8-16GB, 4 concurrent, 4-8 t/s
    42→    LARGE = (16, 32, 2)  # 16-32GB, 2 concurrent, 2-4 t/s
    43→    ULTRA = (32, 128, 1)  # 32-128GB, 1 concurrent, 0.5-2 t/s
    44→
    45→
    46→@dataclass
    47→class ModelConfig:
    48→    """Quantization-aware model configuration"""
    49→
    50→    name: str
    51→    size_gb: float
    52→    quantization: str
    53→    context_max: int
    54→    expected_tps: float  # tokens per second
    55→    cache_hit_rate: float  # L3 cache efficiency
    56→    template_format: str
    57→    optimal_for_ide: list[IDEPriority]
    58→
    59→
    60→class MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer:
    61→    """Real-time memory bandwidth analysis for optimal routing"""
    62→
    63→    def __init__(self):
    64→        self.total_memory_gb = psutil.virtual_memory().total / (1024**3)
    65→        self.available_memory_gb = psutil.virtual_memory().available / (1024**3)
    66→        self.l3_cache_mb = 64  # AMD Ryzen AI MAX+ 395 specific
    67→        self.ddr5_bandwidth_gbps = 85  # Estimated DDR5-8000 performance
    68→
    69→    def analyze_memory_pressure(self) -> float:
    70→        """Calculate memory pressure ratio (0-1)"""
    71→        return 1.0 - (self.available_memory_gb / self.total_memory_gb)
    72→
    73→    def calculate_optimal_concurrent_models(self, model_tier: ModelTier) -> int:
    74→        """Calculate optimal concurrent models based on memory bandwidth"""
    75→        memory_pressure = self.analyze_memory_pressure()
    76→
    77→        if memory_pressure < 0.3:
    78→            return model_tier.value[2]  # Full concurrent capacity
    79→        elif memory_pressure < 0.7:
    80→            return max(1, model_tier.value[2] // 2)  # Reduced capacity
    81→        else:
    82→            return 1  # Conservative single model
    83→
    84→    def estimate_tokens_per_second(self, model_config: ModelConfig) -> float:
    85→        """Estimate tokens/second based on memory bandwidth and cache efficiency"""
    86→        base_bandwidth_factor = self.ddr5_bandwidth_gbps / 100  # Normalize to 1.0
    87→        cache_bonus = (
    88→            model_config.cache_hit_rate * 0.3
    89→        )  # L3 cache provides up to 30% boost
    90→        quantization_factor = self.get_quantization_factor(model_config.quantization)
    91→
    92→        estimated_tps = (
    93→            model_config.expected_tps
    94→            * base_bandwidth_factor
    95→            * (1 + cache_bonus)
    96→            * quantization_factor
    97→        )
    98→
    99→        # Apply memory pressure scaling
   100→        memory_pressure = self.analyze_memory_pressure()
   101→        if memory_pressure > 0.8:
   102→            estimated_tps *= 0.7  # Degraded performance under pressure
   103→        elif memory_pressure > 0.6:
   104→            estimated_tps *= 0.85
   105→
   106→        return estimated_tps
   107→
   108→    def get_quantization_factor(self, quantization: str) -> float:
   109→        """Get performance multiplier based on quantization level"""
   110→        factors = {
   111→            "Q8_0": 1.0,  # Baseline
   112→            "Q6_K": 1.15,  # 15% speed improvement
   113→            "Q4_K_M": 1.35,  # 35% improvement (optimal for CPU)
   114→            "Q3_K_M": 1.5,  # 50% improvement but quality loss
   115→        }
   116→        return factors.get(quantization, 1.0)
   117→

     1→"""
     2→Ollama Model Manager - Benchmark, auto-swap, and storage management.
     3→
     4→Handles:
     5→- Model benchmarking per task type
     6→- Automatic swapping of underperformers
     7→- Storage cleanup of unused models
     8→- Landscape research for new models
     9→"""
    10→
    11→import json
    12→import logging
    13→import time
    14→from dataclasses import dataclass
    15→from datetime import datetime
    16→from pathlib import Path
    17→from typing import Any

    25→
    26→
    27→@dataclass
    28→class ModelMetrics:
    29→    """Metrics for a single model."""
    30→
    31→    name: str
    32→    task_type: str  # analysis, critique, synthesis, function_call, vision
    33→    avg_latency_ms: float = 0.0
    34→    success_rate: float = 1.0
    35→    quality_score: float = 0.5  # 0-1, from critique feedback
    36→    total_calls: int = 0
    37→    last_used: str = ""
    38→    confidence_score: float = 0.0  # 0-1, dynamic confidence estimate
    39→    confidence_samples: int = 0  # Number of samples for confidence calculation
    40→
    41→    def update(self, latency_ms: float, success: bool, quality: float = 0.5):
    42→        n = self.total_calls
    43→        self.avg_latency_ms = (self.avg_latency_ms * n + latency_ms) / (n + 1)
    44→        self.success_rate = (self.success_rate * n + (1.0 if success else 0.0)) / (
    45→            n + 1
    46→        )
    47→        self.quality_score = (self.quality_score * n + quality) / (n + 1)
    48→        self.total_calls += 1
    49→        self.last_used = datetime.now().isoformat()
    50→
    51→        # Update confidence score using Bayesian updating
    52→        if n > 0:
    53→            # Confidence is based on success rate and quality score
    54→            self.confidence_score = self.success_rate * 0.6 + self.quality_score * 0.4
    55→            # Apply sample size adjustment - more samples = higher confidence
    56→            sample_adjustment = min(1.0, n / 50.0)  # Cap at 50 samples
    57→            self.confidence_score *= sample_adjustment
    58→        else:
    59→            self.confidence_score = 0.0
    60→
    61→        self.confidence_samples = n + 1
    62→
    63→
    64→@dataclass
    65→class ModelConfig:
    66→    """Configuration for model roles."""
    67→
    68→    role: str
    69→    primary: str
    70→    fallback: str | None = None
    71→    min_quality: float = 0.6

Reuses `MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer` from `dynamic_model_router.py` (line 60).\n\n```python\nclass ModelTierPolicy(Enum):\n    HOT = \"hot\"    # Always loaded, keep_alive=-1\n    WARM = \"warm\"  # Loaded at startup, evictable under pressure\n    COLD = \"cold\"  # On-demand only, evicted after timeout\n\n@dataclass\nclass PooledModel:\n    name: str\n    tier: ModelTierPolicy\n    size_gb: float\n    loaded: bool = False\n    healthy: bool = False\n    last_used: float = 0.0\n    error_count: int = 0\n    avg_latency_ms: float = 0.0\n\nclass ModelPoolManager:\n    \"\"\"Lifecycle manager for 3-tier hot/warm/cold model rotation.\"\"\"\n\n    async def initialize(self) -> None           # Query /api/tags, reconcile with tier config\n    async def ensure_loaded(self, model) -> bool  # Load if needed, evict cold if pressured\n    async def health_check(self, model) -> bool   # Ping with trivial prompt, update status\n    async def health_check_all(self) -> dict      # Check all loaded models\n    def get_available_models(self) -> list         # Loaded + healthy models\n    async def evict_model(self, model) -> bool     # Unload via keep_alive=0\n    async def promote(self, model, tier) -> None   # cold→warm, warm→hot\n    async def demote_under_pressure(self) -> list  # Evict lowest-priority when memory > 80%\n    def get_pool_status(self) -> PoolStatus        # Summary snapshot\n```\n\nDefault tier config matches `scripts/preload_models.sh`:\n- HOT: phi4-mini-reasoning, nomic-embed-text\n- WARM: glm-4.7-flash, qwen3-coder:30b\n- COLD: gpt-oss:20b, deepcoder:14b, nemotron-3-nano\n\nUses httpx (already in deps) for Ollama API. Reuses `MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer.analyze_memory_pressure()`.\n\n### 2. `src/cohezion/swarm/model_pool_config.py` (~40 lines)\n\nPydantic config + PoolStatus dataclass:\n```python\nclass TierConfig(BaseModel):\n    hot_models: list[str]\n    warm_models: list[str]\n    cold_models: list[str]\n    max_concurrent_loaded: int = 4\n    health_check_interval_s: float = 300.0\n    memory_pressure_threshold: float = 0.80\n    promotion_threshold_calls: int = 10\n\nclass PoolStatus(BaseModel):\n    loaded_models: list[str]\n    healthy_models: list[str]\n    total_memory_gb: float\n    memory_pressure: float\n    models: dict[str, dict]  # name → PooledModel as dict\n```\n\n### 3. `tests/swarm/test_model_pool_manager.py` (~200 lines, 12+ tests)\n\nMock all httpx calls. Test classes:\n- `TestPooledModel` — creation, health transitions\n- `TestModelPoolManager` — initialize, ensure_loaded, health_check, evict, promote, demote\n- `TestPoolManagerIntegration` — integration with CostAwareRouter\n- `TestPoolManagerEdgeCases` — all unhealthy, 100% memory, Ollama unreachable\n\n### 4. `src/cohezion/skills/MODEL_POOL_MANAGEMENT_PRIME.md`\n\nPRIME skill definition codifying the pool management pattern.\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n### 5. `src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py`\n\nAdd optional `pool_manager` parameter (backward-compatible):\n```python\ndef __init__(self, ..., pool_manager: ModelPoolManager | None = None):\n    self._pool_manager = pool_manager\n\ndef select_model(self, query, ...):\n    # ... existing complexity analysis ...\n    # NEW: if pool_manager, check availability before returning\n    if self._pool_manager:\n        available = {m.name for m in self._pool_manager.get_available_models()}\n        if primary_model not in available:\n            primary_model = self._find_available_fallback(primary_model, available)\n```\n\n### 6. `src/cohezion/swarm/__init__.py` — add exports\n### 7. `tests/conftest.py` — add ModelPoolManager singleton reset\n\n## Model Assignment Rationale\n\n| Task | Model | Why |\n|------|-------|-----|\n| T1 Data models | phi4-mini-reasoning | Simple dataclasses/enums, 70 t/s fast |\n| T2 Architecture | glm-4.7-flash | Design reasoning, 0.90 quality |\n| T3 Core impl | qwen3-coder:30b | 200+ lines async Python, code gen strength |\n| T4 Health/promotion | qwen3-coder:30b | Complex state machine logic |\n| T5 Router integration | qwen3-coder:30b | Modifying existing async code |\n| T6 Tests | phi4-mini-reasoning | Follow established test patterns |\n\n## Adversarial Review Protocol\n\n### Tier 1 — Quick Lint (Claude haiku)\n\nChecks: import hygiene, type hints, docstrings, test count >= 12, obvious errors (missing await, bare except). Fast — under 60s.\n\n**Pass:** Zero blockers.\n\n### Tier 2 — Deep Code Review (Claude sonnet)\n\nChecks:\n1. Concurrency safety (race conditions in ensure_loaded?)\n2. Error handling (Ollama unreachable? model load fails?)\n3. Resource leaks (httpx clients closed?)\n4. Edge cases (all unhealthy, 100% memory, model stuck, unknown model)\n5. Backward compatibility (CostAwareRouter change breaks nothing?)\n6. Security (model name injection? timeouts enforced?)\n\n**Pass:** Zero critical issues.\n\n### Tier 3 — Architectural Review (Claude opus)\n\nChecks:\n1. Compound engineering (does this make future features easier?)\n2. HIHO coherence (are tier transitions reversible?)\n3. Charter compliance (observable, deterministic, honest metrics)\n4. Integration coherence with DynamicModelRouter, OllamaModelManager, DynamicConcurrencyGate\n5. Journey tracking (should pool emit 12D events?)\n6. Token efficiency (health check overhead justified?)\n\n**Pass:** No blocking concerns. Charter = ALIGNED. Compound score >= 0.7.\n\n### Combined Verdict\n\n```\nALL PASS → PASS (merge ready)\nANY CONDITIONAL_PASS → CONDITIONAL_PASS (fix conditions, then merge)\nANY FAIL → FAIL (rework required)\n```\n\n## Verification\n\n```bash\nuv run pytest tests/swarm/test_model_pool_manager.py -v        # New tests pass\nuv run pytest tests/swarm/ -q                                   # No swarm regressions\nuv run python -c \"from cohezion.swarm.model_pool_manager import ModelPoolManager\"  # Import OK\n```\n\n## Key Integration Points\n\n- `src/cohezion/swarm/dynamic_model_router.py:60` — `MemoryBandwidthAnalyzer` (reuse for memory pressure)\n- `src/cohezion/swarm/model_manager.py:26` — `OLLAMA_HOST = \"http://localhost:11434\"` (reuse constant)\n- `src/cohezion/swarm/cost_aware_router.py:230` — `select_model()` complexity→model mapping (add pool check)\n- `scripts/preload_models.sh` — canonical tier assignments (pool config must match)\n- `tests/conftest.py` — singleton reset pattern (add ModelPoolManager)\n"
}
